patippuri
tamiḻukkum tamiḻarukkum vaḷamum valimiyum cērkkak
kūṭiya paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷiyellām tēṭiyeṭuttut tamiḻ kūṟum ulakiṟku vaḻaṅkum tolinōkkup pārviyuṭaṉ  tamiḻmaṇ patippakam toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu. ataṉ vāyilāka moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇariṉ nūṟṟāṇṭu niṉivāka avar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ aṉittiyum cērttu avaruṭiya maṟivukkup piṟaku 2000-ttilum, palvēṟu itaḻkaḷilum, malarkaḷilum veḷivantu, nūl vaṭivam peṟāta pāvāṇariṉ ariya kaṭṭurikaḷi ellām tokuttu 2001- lum orucēra veḷiyiṭṭu ulakeṅkum uḷḷa tamiḻarkaḷukku vaḻaṅkiṉōm.  pāvāṇar vaḻi nili aṟiñarkaḷāṉa mutumuṉivar 
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷum, maruttuvar ku.pūṅkāvaṉam avarkaḷum ivvariya kaṭṭurip putiyalkaḷ nūl vaṭivam peṟuvataṟku mikavum uṟutuṇiyāka iruntaṉar. ipperumakkaḷi naṉṟiyuṇarvuṭaṉ niṉivukūrkiṟēṉ.
collārāyccit tuṟiyil tēvanēyar oppaṟṟa taṉit
tiṟamiyuṭivar eṉṟu maṟimaliyaṭikaḷum, nāvalan tīvukku  nantamiḻē tāyeṉṟu pāvēntarum, tamiḻukkum tamiḻ
nāṭṭukkum naṟṟoṇṭu āṟṟiyavar pāvāṇar eṉṟu pēraṟiñar aṇṇā avarkaḷum, veṭṭa veṭṭak kiṭikkum taṅkac
curaṅkam pōṉṟavar eṉṟu tamiḻiṟiñar irācamāṇikkaṉārum, tamiḻakam moḻittuṟiyilē pāvāṇar pōṉṟa oru aṟiñari iṉṉum peṟṟut taravilli eṉṟu paṉmoḻip pulavar
appātturiyarum, kuṟimatiyar tēkkivitta kariyiruḷi nīkka vanta maṟimaliyār vaḻivanta niṟimaliyār pāvāṇar eṉṟu mēṉāḷ pēravittalivar tamiḻkuṭimakaṉ avarkaḷum, tamiḻar yār? etirikaḷ yār? eṉṟu āyntu aṟintu kāṭṭiyavar pāvāṇar eṉṟu pērāciriyar iḷavaracu avarkaḷum,  ōrāyiram āṇṭukaḷ ōyntu kiṭantapiṉ vārātu pōla vanta māmaṇi pāvāṇar eṉṟu mutumuṉivar iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷa  perumikaḷukkuriya pēraṟiñariṉ nūlkaḷi mīḷpatippaka veḷiyiṭuvatil perumi koḷkiṟēṉ.
pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ attaṉiyum tamiḻmoḻikku ēṟṟam urippaṉa. tamiḻi āriya iruḷiṉiṉṟu mīṭṭuk kāppaṉa. vīḻntu
paṭṭa tamiḻaṉukku viḻippūṭṭa vallaṉa. āyiramāyiram āṇṭukaḷāka aṭimippaṭṭukkiṭakkum tamiḻmoḻiyi mīṭkavallatu eṉṟu  pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār avarkaḷiṉ kūṟṟi ik kaḷañciya veḷiyīṭṭil pativu ceyvatu ematu kaṭamiyākum.
pāvāṇarit tūkkip piṭittāltāṉ tamiḻiṉam urup
paṭamuṭiyum - uyaramuṭiyum. pāvāṇar koḷkikaḷ tamiḻar uḷḷamellām nilittu niṟpataṟkum, pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ tamiḻar illamelām iṭam peṟuvataṟkum muṉ kuṟippiṭṭa 2000 - 2001 kālakkaṭṭattil veḷiyiṭappaṭṭa pāvāṇariṉ aṟivuk karuvūlaṅkaḷiyellām oṭṭu mottamāka cērttu orē vīccil pāvāṇar tamiḻk kaḷañciyam eṉum talippil  em aṟakkaṭṭaḷi veḷiyiṭukiṟatu.
maṟikkappeṟṟa māperum varalāṟṟiyum, citikkap peṟṟa oppuyarvuyaṟṟa moḻiyiyum koṇṭa tamiḻiṉattiṉ muṉṉēṟṟam karuti ivvaruntamiḻ putiyalkaḷi tamiḻ kūṟum nallulakiṟku  vaḻaṅka muṉ vantuḷḷōm. tamiḻ moḻiyi mūccākavum, pēccākavum, ceyalākavum koṇṭu orunāḷiṉ muḻuppoḻutum tamiḻākavē vāḻnta cempulaccemmal,  taṉittamiḻk katiravaṉ moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar nūlkaḷi vāṅkip payaṉ koḷvīr.
iḷamiyilēyē potuttoṇṭilum, taṉittamiḻ iyakkat toṇṭilum nāṉ īṭupāṭu koḷvataṟkuk kāraṇamāka iruntavarkaḷ perumpulavar nakkīraṉ avarkaḷum, avartam iḷaval pulavar cittiravēlaṉ avarkaḷum āvar. ivarkaḷi ivveḷiyiṭṭiṉ vāyilāka naṉṟiyuṇarvōṭu niṉivu kūṟukiṟēṉ.  tanti periyāriṉ taṉmatippu iyakkak koḷki
kaḷālum, moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇariṉ taṉittamiḻ iyakkak koḷkikaḷālum īrkkappaṭṭavaṉ. attaku piṉpulattōṭu patippuppaṇiyil eṉ kālaṭic cuvaṭukaḷip patittu varukiṟēṉ.
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ,
patippāḷar.

kuṟi viḷakkam
+	itu puṇarkuṟi
=	itu camakkuṟi
-	itu valappuṟattiluḷḷatu moḻipeyarppu, poruḷ, tiripu eṉṉum mūṉṟaṉuḷ oṉṟu eṉpatik kuṟikkum
<	itu valappuṟattiluḷḷatu mūlam eṉpatik kuṟikkum
x	itu etirmaṟik kuṟi
	itu mēṟkōṭ kuṟiyallāviṭattu mēṟpaṭikkuṟi
( )	itu cila iṭaṅkaḷil vaḻimuṟit tirivik kuṟikkum.
	vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa ‘ri’ eṉṉum uyireḻuttu, ip puttakattil 
‘ru’ eṉṟeḻutappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.
ip puttakattiṟkup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭa āṅkilac colliyalakarātikaḷ kīṟṟum (Skeat) cēmparārum (Chambers) eḻutiyavi. āṅkāṅkut tolkāppiya nūṟpāviṟkuk kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa eṇ, ci. ci. nū. pa. ka. acciṭṭa tolkāppiya mūlattiṉpaṭiyatu.

oppiyaṉ moḻinūl - 2

2
paṇṭit tamiḻakam
aḻintupōṉa tamiḻnūlkaḷ
ēṉiya moḻikaḷilellām ilakkiyam varavara uyarntum mikuntum varavum, tamiḻilō varavarat tāḻntum kuṟintum vantirukkiṉṟatu. vaṭamoḻi teṉmoḻi yilakkiyaṅkaḷ iru peruṅkaṭalkaḷākat toṉṉūlkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, vaṭamoḻikkaṭal muṉṉuḷḷapaṭiyē iṉṟum kuṟiyātuḷatu. āṉāl, teṉmoḻikkaṭalō oru ciṟu kuḷamāka vaṟṟiyuḷḷatu.
akattiyarukku muntiya tamiḻnūlkaḷil, ceṅkōṉ tariccelavu eṉṉum ciṟiya nūliṉ oru pakutiyē iṉṟu kiṭittuḷatu.
talikkaḻakattārāṟ pāṭappaṭṭa ettuṇiyō paripāṭalum, mutunāriyum mutukurukum, kaḷariyāviriyumeṉa ittoṭakkattaṉavum iṭikkaḻakattārāṟ pāṭappaṭṭa kaliyum, kurukum, veṇṭāḷiyum, viyāḻamāliyakava lumeṉa it toṭakkattaṉavum kaṭikkaḻakattārāṟ pāṭappaṭṭa paripāṭaluṭ palavum, kūttum variyum ciṟṟiciyum, pēriciyum ip pōtilli.
talikkaḻakakkālat tilakkaṇamākiya akattiyamum, iṭik kaḻakat tilakkaṇamākiya māpurāṇamum icinūlum pūta purāṇamum ippōtilli.

pāṇṭiyaṉ tamiḻppāratam, tiruccirāppaḷḷiyantāti, srīrāja rāja vijayam, nāṭakanūl, vīraṇukka vijayam, kulōttuṅka cōḻa cariti, aṣṭātaca purāṇam, aruṇili vicākaṉ tamiḻp pāratam, peruvañci, attikirit tirumāl cintu, kāṅkēyaṉ piḷḷikkavi, vīramāli, tiruvatikik kalampakam, tiru valliyantāti mutaliyavi, ituvari yaṟiyappaṭāta nūlkaḷākac cāsaṉat tamiḻkkavi caritattiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
kaṭikkaḻakattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭa pulavarkaḷiyaṟṟiya nūlkaḷiṟ pala ippōtu kiṭikkavilli. oṭṭakkūttariyaṟṟiya arumpit toḷḷāyiram, etirnūl mutaliyavi iṉṉum veḷi varavilli.
iṭikkaḻaka virukkiyākiya kapāṭapurattil, eṇṇā yirat teccam nūlkaḷiruntu piṉpu kaṭalkōḷuṇṭaḻintaṉa veṉṟoru vaḻimuṟi vaḻakkuḷḷatu.

akattiyar
akattiyar eṉṟu ettaṉiyō pēruḷar. avaruḷ mutalāvārē iṅkuk kūṟappaṭupavar.
akattiyar kālam
akattiyar irāmar kālattavarātaliṉ, ēṟattāḻak ki.mu. 1200 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭavarāvar.
akattiyar katikaḷ
(1) vintattiṉ cerukkaṭakkiṉatu
orukālattil paṉimali (imayam) nīrkkīḻ iruntatu. appōtu vintiyamali mikavuyaramāyt tōṉṟiṟṟu. paṉimali yeḻuntapiṉ, ataṉoṭu oppunōkka vintam mikac ciṟitāyt tōṉṟiṟṟu. itē vintattiṉ cerukkaṭakkam. iti akattiyar peyaroṭu btāṭuttukTṟakfraṇam,mvarbtaṉṉāṭutntapiṉFmarināṭṭikfllfṇṭamiyē.af°âa® eṉṉum peyarukku, vintattiṉ akatti (cerukki) aṭakki ṉavar eṉṟu, vaṭamoḻiyiṟ poruḷ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
(2) kaṭalik kuṭittatu
muṉpu kaṭalāyirunta pākam piṉpu nilamākip paṉimali tōṉṟiyatiyē, akattiyar kaṭalik kuṭittatākak kati kaṭṭiyatākat terikiṉṟatu.
(3) kuṭattiṟ piṟantatu

kuṭṭi - kuṭṭam. o.nō paṭṭi - paṭṭam, taṭṭi - taṭṭam.
ceyyuḷaṭikaḷ kuṟaḷuñ cintumāyk kuṟuki varuvatu kuṭṭam paṭutal eṉṟum, kikāl viralkaḷ aḻukik kuṭṭiyākum 
toḻunōyum kuraṅkukkuṭṭiyuṅ kuṭṭameṉṟuṅ kūṟappaṭutal kāṇka.
kuṭṭattik kuṣṭameṉpar vaṭanūlār. o.nō. nfh£l« nfhZl« (t.), muṭṭi - muṣṭi (va.).
cila peyarkaḷil, am eṉṉum muṉṉoṭṭuc cērtal iyalpu.
kā: aṅkayaṟkaṇṇi, aṅkāḷammi.
iṅṅaṉamē kuṭṭamuṉi eṉṉum peyarum aṅkuṭṭamuṉi eṉṟu ākiyirukkalām.
aṅkuṭṭaṉ eṉṟu ēṟkeṉavē peruviralukkup peyar. kuṭṭiyāyiruppatāl kaṭṭiviral kuṭṭaṉ eṉappaṭṭatu. aḵṟiṇip peyarum aṉ īṟu peṟum. kā : kuṭṭaṉ = āṭṭukkuṭṭi. kuṭṭāṉ = ciṟupeṭṭi, ciṟu paṭappu. am + kuṭṭaṉ = aṅkuṭṭaṉ. itu vaṭamoḻiyil aṅkuṣṭaṉ eṉappaṭum.
akattiyar peyarāṉa aṅkuṭṭaṉ eṉpatiyum, peru viraliṉ peyarāṉa aṅkuṭṭaṉ eṉpatiyum, oṉṟāy mayakki, akattiyar aṅkuṭṭa (peruviral) aḷaviṉar eṉṟu paḻimiyar kūṟa nērntirukkalām.
(5) mānilattic camaṉākkiyatu
paṉimali orukālattil nīrkkīḻiruntu piṉpu veḷit tōṉṟiyatu. akattiyar teṟkē vantapiṉ, kumarināṭṭik kaṭalkoṇṭatu. muṟkālattil muṟiyē tāḻntum uyarntu mirunta vaṭateṉnilappākaṅkaḷ, piṟkālattil uyarntum tāḻntum pōyiṉa. iv viraṇṭiyum iṇittu, akattiyar tāḻntum uyarntumirunta vaṭateṉ nilappākaṅkaḷic camappaṭuttiṉareṉṟum, vaṭapākam amiḻntataṟkuk kāraṇam nilattiṟkup pāramākumāṟu patiṉeṇ kaṇattavar vantu 
kūṭiya civaperumāṉ kaliyāṇameṉṟum katikaṭṭiṉar paḻimiyar.
(6) tamiḻi uṇṭākkiṉatu
taÄœ mikap paḻimiyāṉatātalālum, piṟkālat tamiḻarkkuc carittiravaṟiviṉmiyālum. itupōtu aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟa tamiḻ nūlkaḷuḷ akattiyam muṉṉatātalālum, akattiyar talikkaḻakattiṟutiyil vantavarātalālum akattiyar tamiḻi uṇṭākkiṉar eṉṟu cilar karutalāyiṉar.
akattiyam vaḻinūlātal
akattiyam tamiḻilakkaṇa mutaṉūleṉṟu, ituvariyum kūṟappaṭṭuvantatu. iṟṟiyārāycciyāl atu vaḻinūlē yeṉpatu teḷḷatteḷiyat terikiṉṟatu.

(1) tolkāppiyattil ōriṭattum akattiyam kūṟavum cuṭṭavum paṭāmi.
(2) eṉpa, eṉmaṉār pulavar, eṉa moḻipa, eṉṟiciṉōrē eṉṟu tolkāppiyar muṉṉūlāciriyarip pallōrākak kuṟittal.

ēḻiyaṉ muṟiya tetirmuka vēṟṟumi
 vēṟeṉa viḷampāṉ peyaratu vikārameṉ
 ṟōtiya pulavaṉu muḷaṉoru vakiyā
 ṉintira ṉeṭṭām vēṟṟumi yeṉṟaṉaṉ
eṉṟu akattiyar tamakku muntiṉa iru nūlāciriyari vitantuṅ kūṟiṉar.
akattiyattiṟkumuṉ, intiraṉ eṉṟoru pulavaṉ iyaṟṟiya aintiram eṉṉum ilakkaṇam iruntami,
malkunīr varippiṉ aintira niṟinta
 tolkāp piyaṉeṉat taṉpeyar tōṟṟi
eṉṟu tolkāppiyap pāyirattiṟ kūṟiyatā laṟiyalām.
“ēḻiyaṉ muṟiyatu eṉṉum akattiya nūṟpāvil kuṟikkappaṭṭa iru nūlkaḷiyum muṟiyē vaṭamoḻi ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷākiya pāṇiṉīyamum aintiramumeṉak koṇṭaṉar pallōr. iJ tamiḻiṉ toṉmiyi aṟiyāmiyāl nērnta tavaṟākum.
pāṇiṉi ki.mu. 5ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉarā yirukka, avariyaṟ ṟiya ilakkaṇam eṅṅaṉam irāmar kālattavarāṉa akattiyar nūlukku mutaṉūlātal cellum?
aintiram eṉpatu intiraṉ eṉṟōr (āriyap) pulavar iyaṟṟiya ilakkaṇamākum. akattiyarkku muṉṉamē orucila āriyar teṉṉāṭṭiṟku vantiruntami, aṟuvakip paṭṭa pārppaṉap pakkamum eṉṟu tolkāppiyar kūṟuvatālum, kantapurāṇattālum, tirumāliṉ mutalintu tōṟṟaravuc ceytikaḷālu maṟiyappaṭum.
aintiram eṉpatu vaṭamoḻi yilakkaṇamā teṉmoḻi yilakkaṇamā eṉṟu tiṭṭamāyk kūṟa iyalavilli. āyiṉum, intiraṉ - aintiram eṉṉum tiripākupeyar (tattitānta) muṟiyiṉālum, aintirattiṉum vēṟāka muntunūl kaṇṭu eṉac cila nūlkaḷip paṉampāraṉār kuṟittalālum 
atu vaṭamoḻi yilakkaṇamē yeṉak koḷḷa iṭamuṇṭu.

eṉṟu cilappatikārattil, nāṭukāṇ kātiyiṟ kūṟiṉatu meṉka.
aintiram niṟinta tolkāppiyaṉ eṉṟu tolkāp piyar ciṟappikkappaṭuvatāl, aintiram mika viḻumiya nūleṉpatu peṟappaṭum. intiraṉ marutanilattup paṇṭit tamiḻt teyvamātaliṉ, aintiram ataṉ ciṟappuppaṟṟiyum nūlāciriyaṉ peyaroppumipaṟṟiyum at teyvattiṉatākak kūṟappaṭṭatu. iṟiyaṉār akapporuḷ ataṉ nūlāciriyaṉ peya
roppumipaṟṟiyum, kōyiṟ pīṭattaṭiyiṟ kiṭantamipaṟṟiyum, civaperumā ṉiyaṟṟiyatākak karutappaṭṭami kāṇka. intiraṉuk kup paṇṭit tamiḻppeyar vēntaṉ eṉpatu. intira teyvattiṟkup paṇṭit tamiḻnāṭṭilirunta perumiyic cilappatikārattāṉum maṇimēkaliyāṉu muṇarka.
tamiḻil 8 vēṟṟumiyum orē kālattil tōṉṟiṉavi yalla. mutalāvatu 5 allatu 6 vēṟṟumikaḷtām tōṉṟi yirukka muṭiyum. iṟutiyil tōṉṟiṉatu eṭṭām vēṟṟumi. atu oru kālattil mutal vēṟṟumiyiṉ vēṟupāṭeṉṟu ataṉuḷ aṭakkappaṭṭatu. itiyē,
ēḻiyaṉ muṟiya tetirmuka vēṟṟumi
 vēṟeṉa viḷampāṉ peyaratu vikārameṉ
 ṟōtiya pulavaṉu muḷaṉ
eṉpatu kuṟikkum. āṅkilattilum viḷivēṟṟumiyi mutal vēṟṟumiyiṉ vēṟupāṭu (Nominative of Address) eṉṟē kūṟuvar.
aintira ilakkaṇattil, viḷivēṟṟumi muṉṉūlkaḷiṟ pōla mutal vēṟṟumiyil aṭakkappaṭātu, taṉi vēṟṟumi yākak kūṟappaṭṭatu. itiyē,

(4) akattiyam muttamiḻilakkaṇamātal
oru moḻiyil, mutaṉmuta lilakkaṇam tōṉṟumpōtu, kūṭiya pakkam eḻuttu col yāppu eṉṟa mūṉṟukkē tōṉṟum. akattiyattil im mūṉṟuṭaṉ poruḷilakkaṇamum, ataṉ mēlum ici nāṭaka vilakkaṇaṅkaḷum kūṟappaṭṭiruttaliṉ, atu mutaṉūlāyirukka muṭiyāteṉpatu tēṟṟam.
akattiyattiṟku āriyavilakkaṇat toṭarpiṉmi
akattiyam vaṭamoḻi yilakkaṇa nūlkaṭku mikamika muntiyatātalāṉum, tamiḻilakkaṇattiluḷḷa kuṟiyīṭukaḷellām centamiḻc coṟkaḷē yātalāṉum, vaṭamoḻi yilakkaṇat tilillāta cila collamitikaḷum poruḷilakkaṇamum ici nāṭakavilakkaṇamum tamiḻiluṇmiyāṉum, āriyar intiyāviṟ kuḷ puku muṉpē tirāviṭar taliciṟanta nākarikam aṭintirunta miyāṉum. akattiyattiṟkum vaṭamoḻi yilakkaṇaṅkaṭkum eṭṭuṇiyum iyipiṉ ṟeṉka.
āyuṅ kuṇattava lōkitaṉ pakka lakattiyaṉkēṭ
 ṭēyum puvaṉik kiyampiya taṇṭami ḻīṅkurikka
eṉṟu puttamittiraṉ kūṟiyatu, tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ puttattic ciṟappittaṟkuk kūṟiya puṉinturiyē yākum.
tamiḻilakkiyattiṉ uccanilik kālam 
(The Augustan Age of Tamil Literature)
tamiḻilakkiyattiṉ uccanilikkālam talikkaḻakak kālamē yeṉpataṟkuk kāraṇaṅkaḷ:
(1) tamiḻp payiṟci varavarak kuṟital
talikkaḻakak kālattil, tamiḻ muttamiḻāyiruntu, piṉpu iṭikkaḻakak kālattil vevvēṟāyp pirintatu. kaṭikkaḻakak kālattil ici nāṭaka ülkaḷiruntumòlavarāṟbparumpālum,gyilappaṭavilli.ïJnghnjh, ici nāṭaka nūlkaḷ illāmiyuṭaṉ, iyaṟṟamiḻum cariyāyk kaṟkappaṭavilli, iyaṟṟamiḻp pakutikaḷil mukkiyamāṉa poruḷilakkaṇam terinta pulavar itupōtu mikac cilarēyāvar.
(2) mukkaḻakaṅkaḷum muṟiyē oṭuṅkal
mukkaḻakaṅkaḷum kālattiṉālaṉṟit taṉmiyiṉālum 
taliyiṭi kaṭiyāyiṉami, kīḻkkāṇum kuṟippāl viḷaṅkum.

(3) kalikaḷ varavara maṟital
talikkaḻakak kālattil ettuṇiyō kalikaḷ tamiḻiliruntaṉa. avi ovvoṉṟāy maṟintupōyiṉa. iṉṟum nūlvaḻakki lillāviṭiṉum ceyalvaḻakkiṟ pala kalikaḷuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu ciṟpam, ciṟpak kalikkuriya akkirapaṭṭiyal, palaki, muṉi, itaḻ, kuṭam, tāṭi, kāl, nākapantam, pōtiki, yāḷam, kūṭu, nāṇutal, matali, pūmuṉi, koṭi, cāli, kumpam, pīṭam, maṇṭapam, kōpuram, koṭuṅki, curuḷyāḷi, tūṇ, paṭṭam, aḷavu, uttiram, muṭṭi pantam, koṭivaḷi, āḷāṅku, aṇiveṭṭikkāl, kōmukam mutaliya kuṟiyīṭukaḷ iṉṟum tamiḻāyuḷḷaṉa.1 piṟa kuṟiyīṭukaḷum taṟpōtu vaṭacollā yiruppiṉum, jmiḻiṉiṉṟumbmāḻipeyarkkapgṭṭaviyē.ï‹W« tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ kaṭṭaṭat toḻil tamiḻarālēyē ceyyappaṭuvatum pārppaṉarāṟ ceyyappaṭā miyuṅ kāṇka.
(4) ilakkaṇam varavarap piṟaḻtal 
tolkāppiyar kālattilēlē nūlil ilakkaṇap piḻikaḷ tōṉṟiṉami muṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭatu. atōṭu vaḻakkilum pala vaḻūu muṭipukaḷ piṟkālattil tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
alla eṉṉum paṭarkkip palaviṉpāl etirmaṟik kuṟippu viṉimuṟṟē, ēṉi eṇṇiṭaṅkaṭkum vaḻaṅki varutal kāṇka.
(5) coṟkaḷ varavarap poruḷiḻattal
talikkaḻakattiṟkup piṉ, oruporuṭ palacoṟkaḷip pulavarkaḷ perumpālum tiṭṭamillātu vaḻaṅki vantiruk kiṉṟaṉar.
ī, tā, koṭu eṉpaṉa, muṟiyē iḻintōṉ ottōṉ uyarntōṉ coṟkaḷākum. ivi piṟkālattil vēṟupāṭiṉṟi vaḻaṅkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
(6) tamiḻ varavarat tūymikeṭal
tolkāppiyam muḻumiyilum, aintāṟē vaṭacoṟkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. piṟkālattil avi varavarap perukiṉamiyi, muṟiyē, kaṭikkaḻaka nūlkaḷ, tiruvācakam, kamparāmāyaṇam, villiputtūrāḻvār pāratam mutaliyavaṟṟi nōkkik kāṇka.
1	Dravidian Architecture,pp. 10-23
piṟkālattiṟ ceyyuḷ ceytār tamiḻiṉ tūymiyic ciṟituṅ karutāmal, mōṉiyetukiyoṉṟē nōkki, vaṭacoṟkaḷi ērāḷamāyum tārāḷamāyum vaḻaṅkiviṭṭaṉar.
(7) nūṟceyyuḷ varavara iḻital
mukkaḻakak kālaṅkaḷilum, pākkaḷālēyē perumpālum nūlka ḷiyaṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. piṟkālattār perumpālum pāviṉaṅ kaḷiyē terintukoṇṭaṉar, pāviṉum pāviṉam iyaṟṟutaṟ keḷitāyiruttaliṉ.
(8) poruḷiṉum collē varavarac ciṟattal
kaḻakak kālaṅkaḷil colliṉum poruḷē ciṟanta teṉṟu, centoṭiyāyiṉum poruḷ ciṟappac ceyyuḷ ceytaṉar. piṟkālattil, maṭakku, tiripu mutaliya collaṇi kaḷiyum, palavaki ōviya(cittira)c ceyyuḷkaḷiyum ciṟappākak koṇṭu, avaṟṟilēyē tam tiṟamiyik kāṭṭiṉar. poruḷaṇiyil iyaṟkik karuttum collaṇiyil ceyaṟkik karuttum amital, ceyyuḷiyaṟṟip payiṉṟavar yāvarkkum pulaṉām.
(9) nūṟporuḷ varavara iḻital
muṟkālap pulavar mēṉāṭṭārpōlap palvakik kali nūlkaḷi iyaṟṟiṉar; piṟkālap pulavarō kōvi, ulā, antāti,  kalampakam mutaliya pukaḻnūl vakikaḷiyē iyaṟṟuvā
rāyiṉar. oruvar kōvi pāṭiviṭṭāl, taliciṟanta pulavarāka matikkappaṭuvar. yāviyum pāṭik kōviyip pāṭu eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.
piṟkālattil vakutta 96 paṉuvalkaḷum perumpālum pukaḻ nūlvakikaḷē. itaṉāl piṟkālattāratu kaliyuṇarcciyiṉmi veḷiyākum.
ceyyuḷkaṭkum nūlaḷavukkum kulamuṟi vakuttatum, piṟkālattār pullaṟivāṇmiyik kāṭṭum.
kaṭikkaḻakak kālattil nālvar taṉittaṉi nāṟpatu ceyyuḷkoṇṭa nūloṉṟi yiyaṟṟiṉar. piṟkālattup pāṭṭiyalkaḷ avaṟṟiṉ tokiyiliruntum, nāṉāṟpatu eṉṟoru nūlvakiyi vakuttukkoṇṭatu nakippiṟ kiṭamāṉatē. kaliyuṇarcci yiḻantamiyālum, vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉ ṟum vanta paḻamik kalviyiṉālum, carittiram tiṇinūl eṉṉum īr aṟiviyaṟkalikaṭkum māṟākap pāviyaṅ(kāviyam)kaḷil, nāṭṭup paṭalam nakarap paṭalam mutaliya pakutikaḷil, tam mati nuṭpattik kāṭṭuvatoṉṟē kuṟikkōḷākak koṇṭu, poyyum puliyumāṉavaṟṟiyellām puṉivārāyiṉar piṟkālap pulavar.
(10) āṭpeyarkaḷ varavara vaṭacollātal
kaṭikkaḻakattiṟku muṉkālattil, aracar peyarkaḷ kaṭuṅkōṉ kāyciṉaṉ eṉac centamiḻp peyarkaḷā yirun tamiyum, piṉkālattil jaṭilavarmaṉ parākkiramaṉ eṉa vaṭamoḻip peyarkaḷāṉamiyum, paṭittalivaṉ, mēlōṉ eṉṉum taṉittamiḻp peyarkaḷiruppavum, avaṟṟukkup patilāy, muṟiyē ēṉāti (cēṉāpati), eṭṭi (cirēṣṭaṉ) eṉṉum vaṭamoḻip peyarkaḷ paṭṭappeyarkaḷāy vaḻaṅkiṉamiyuṅ kāṇka.
(11) varavara vaṭanūl tamiḻnūluk kaḷaviyātal
(12) tamiḻar palatuṟikaḷilum varavarak keṭṭuvarutal
kaṭikkaḻaka ilakkiyam ōr ilakkiyamākāmi
palar kaṭikkaḻaka ilakkiyamē tamiḻilakkiyam eṉṟu niṉittukkoṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. atuvē ilakkiyamāyiṉ, tamiḻ puṉmoḻikaḷil oṉṟākavē vitteṇṇappaṭum.
patiṉeṇ mēṟkaṇakkāṉa pattuppāṭṭu eṭṭuttoki eṉpavaṟṟuḷ, pattuppāṭṭu, aiṅkuṟunūṟu, patiṟṟuppattu, kalittoki eṉṟa nāṉkē nūl eṉṉum peyarkkuc ciṟitu urimiyuṭiyavi. iviyum pukaḻum akapporuḷum paṟṟiyaviyē ēṉiya nāṉkum taṉippāṭaṟ ṟiraṭṭukaḷ. patiṉeṇ kīḻkkaṇakku nūlkaḷ taṉinūlka ḷāyiṉum, naṉṉeṟi akapporuḷ eṉṉum iru poruḷ paṟṟiyaṉavē.
kaṭikkaḻaka nūlkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum muppattāṟa ṉuḷḷum oṉṟāvatu kalipaṟṟiyataṉṟu, pāviyamumaṉṟu. avaṟṟuḷ ācārakkōviyō vaṭanūl moḻipeyarppāyum piṟappiluyarvu tāḻvu vakuppatāyum, eḷiya poruḷkaḷik kūṟuvatāyumuḷḷatu.
kaṭikkaḻaka nūlkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum muppattāṟum, ak kaḻakak kālattilēyē tōṉṟiyaviyalla. avaṟṟuṭ cila ataṉ piṉṉarē iyaṟṟavum tokukkavumpaṭṭaṉa. toki nūlkaḷel lām oruvarē orē kālattil tokuttaviyumalla.
mēlum, nālaṭiyār, nāṉmaṇikkaṭiki mutaliya pala kīḻkkaṇakku nūlkaḷ, kaṭikkaḻakap pulavarā liyaṟṟappaṭā miyum, mēṟkaṇakku patiṉeṭṭiṟkoppak kīḻkkaṇakku patiṉeṭṭu vakukkappaṭṭamiyum, naṟṟiṇi, kuṟuntoki, akanāṉūṟu, puṟanāṉūṟu eṉpaṉa nannāṉūṟu pāṭalkaḷāyt tokukkappaṭṭamiyum nōkki uṇarntukoḷka. patiṉeṇ kīḻkkaṇakkuḷ patiṉeṭṭāvatu nūl, kinniliyeṉṟum iṉṉiliyeṉṟum iruvēṟu koḷki nilavuvatiyum nōkkuka.
ciṟanta nūlkaḷellām akattiyarkku muṉṉamē tamiḻil iyaṟṟappaṭṭiruntaṉa. piṟkālattār avaṟṟi ārāyntē vantaṉar eṉpati, 
taliccaṅkamiruntār.... tamiḻārāyntatu kaṭal koḷḷap
paṭṭa maturiyeṉpa. iṭiccaṅkamiruntār.... tamiḻārāyntatu kapāṭapuratteṉpa. kaṭiccaṅkamiruntu tamiḻārāyntār.... caṅka miruntu tamiḻārāyntatu uttaramaturi yeṉpa eṉṟu mukkaḻaka varalāṟṟiṟ kūṟiyatiṉiṉṟum aṟintukoḷka.
akattiyarkku muṉṉarē taṉittamiḻar tamiḻ vaḷarttami
akattiyarkku muṉ intiraṉ eṉṟa ōr āriyar, tamiḻilak kaṇam ceytami muṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭatu. av vintiraṉukku muṉ āriyar ilakkaṇam perumpālum ceytirukka muṭiyātu.
taliccaṅkamiruntār.... nālāyirattu nāṉūṟṟu nāṟpatiṟṟi yāṇṭu caṅkamiruntāreṉpa. avarkaḷic caṅkamirīiyiṉār kāyciṉavaḻuti mutalākak kaṭuṅkōṉīṟāka eṇpattoṉpatiṉ mareṉpa eṉṟu, talikkaḻakamirunta kālam 89 araca talimuṟi yiṉatākac collappaṭutalāl, akattiyar avvaḷavu kāla miruntirukka muṭiyāteṉpatu muḻuttēṟṟam. ākavē, talik kaḻakattiṉ iṟutik kālattil, allatu ataṉ piṉtāṉ, akattiyar tamiḻnāṭṭiṟku vantirukkamuṭiyum. avar teṉmaturi ceṉṟa cila āṇṭukaḷukkuḷ, kumarināṭṭiṉ teṉpakutiyik kaṭal koṇṭatu. piṉpu avar ōyvupeṟṟup potiyamaliyil tavamiruntu tam iṟutikkālattik kaḻittār. pāṇṭiyaṉ kapāṭapurattik kaṭṭi aṅku iṭik kaḻakatti niṟuviṉāṉ. atil tolkāppiyar talimi tāṅkiṉār. akattiyar akkālattil iruntaṉarēṉum, ak kaḻakattil uṟuppiṉarā yiruntatākat teriyavilli. iṭikkaḻakattilum avar uṟuppiṉarāyiruntatāka mukkaḻaka varalāṟu kūṟuvatu, avar akkālattiliruntami paṟṟiyēyaṉṟi vēṟaṉṟu. talikkaḻakatti avar amittār eṉṟa varalāṟu poruntāteṉpatu muṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭatu. ovvoru kaḻakamum pala talimuṟiyāka naṭipeṟṟateṉṟum, avaṟṟuḷ iṟutit talimuṟiyilirunta pulavar tokiyē varalāṟṟiṟ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟateṉṟum aṟintu koḷka.
akattiyar, tolkāppiyar kālattiliruntu kaṭikkaḻakak kālattil kapila paraṇa nakkīrarum, aṇmik kālattil ṭākṭar u.vē. cāminātiyar avarkaḷum, rāvcāhip mu. irākavi yaṅkār avarkaḷum, rā. irākaviyaṅkār avarkaḷumākap perumpālum pārppaṉarē tamiḻnūluk katikārikaḷā yiruntu varuvatu evarkkum terintatē.
kumarināṭu evvaḷavu paḻimiyāṉatō avvaḷavu paḻimiyāṉatu tamiḻ eṉpati avar aṟivārāka. toṇṭu, num eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷiṉ iyalpu akattiyar kālattavarāṉa tolkāppiyarāl aṟiyappaṭāmiyālum, ikkālattiṉiṉṟu paḻaṅkālam nōkkic cellaccellat tamiḻil vaṭacoṟkaḷ kuṟintukoṇṭē pōvatiṉālum, akattiyattilum tol kāppiyattilum aintāṟu coṟkaḷē vaṭacoṟkaḷāyirutta talāṉum, akattiyar kālattiṟku muṉpē eṭṭuṇiyum vaṭacoṟ kalavāta taṉittamiḻ vaḻaṅkiyateṉṟum atu tamiḻa rālēyē vaḻaṅkappaṭṭateṉṟum aṟintukoḷka.
pērakattiyam eṉṟu itupōtu vaḻaṅkum nūl piṟ kālattil vaṭamoḻippaṟṟuḷḷa oruvarāṟ ceyyappaṭṭatu. atu pērilmaṭṭum akattiyam eṉka.
akattiyar ciṟṟakattiyam pērakattiyam eṉa iruvēṟu nūlkaḷ iyaṟṟiyatākat teriyavilli. akattiyam vaḻakkaṟṟapiṉ, ataṉ nūṟpāt tiraṭṭukaḷil, ciṟiyatum periyatumāṉa iraṇṭē muṟiyē ap peyarpeṟṟu vaḻaṅkiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
vaṭacollit tamiḻcceyyuṭkuriya coṟkaḷil oṉṟākavum (ecca.1) moḻipeyarppi vaḻinūl vakikaḷuḷ oṉṟākavum (marapu. 94) tolkāppiyar kūṟiyatu porunta villi. avaṟṟuḷ, vaṭacol orōvoṉṟu (vīṇāka) varuvatu avar kālattil uḷḷatēyāyiṉum, moḻipeyarp piṟkut tamiḻil iṭamiruntatākat teriyavilli. āṉālum, tolkāppiyattil aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiyiruppatāl, atu tamiḻi ṉiṉṟum moḻipeyartta vaṭanūlkaḷiyē mutaṉūlkaḷākak kāṭṭum āriya ēmāṟṟu, avarkku muṉṉamē toṭaṅkiṉatiyē uṇarttuvatākum. itu vēṇṭāta vaṭa coṟkaḷ tolkāppi yattil vaḻaṅkuvataṉālum uṇarappaṭum. akkālattil iraṇṭoru coṟkaḷē āriyar tamiḻiṟ pukuttamuṭiyum eṉpatiyum uyttuṇarka.
akattiyarkkumuṉ tamiḻ caṟṟut taḷarntiruntami
akattiyar kālattiṟkumuṉ, tamiḻnūlkaḷ ciṟitupōtu kaṟkappaṭātiruntami, piṉvaruṅ kāraṇaṅkaḷāl viḷaṅkum.
(1) akattiyar murukaṉi nōkkit tavaṅkiṭakka, at teyvam tōṉṟi, avarkkuc cila ōliccuvaṭikaḷik kāṭṭiṟṟu eṉṟa kati.
(2) akattiyar tamiḻi uṇṭākkiṉār eṉṟa vaḻakku.
(3) tolkāppiyap pāyirattil muntunūl kaṇṭu eṉṟu kūṟiyiruttal.
akattiyarkku muṉpē, cila āriyar tamiḻnāṭṭiṟku vantiruntami muṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭatu. avarvayappaṭṭē pāṇṭiyaṉ tamiḻic ciṟitupōtu vaḷarkkātiruntirukka vēṇṭum. kaṭikkaḻattiṟkuppiṉ, pāṇṭiyariruntum kaḻakam niṟuvāmiyum, vaṇaṅkāmuṭimāṟaṉ poyyāmoḻip pulavar kaḻakam niṟuvac coṉṉamikku iṇaṅkāmiyum nōkkiyuṇarka.
tolkāppiyar akattiyarkkut teriyāta cila toṉṉūlkaḷik kaṇṭamipaṟṟiyē, tolkāppiyar eṉṟu avar peyar peṟavum, muntunūl kaṇṭu eṉṟu pāyirattiṟ kuṟikkavum nērntateṉka. iti, perumpulavar mīṉāṭcicuntaram piḷḷiyavarkaḷ kālattil kaḻaka nūlkaḷ aṟiyappaṭātiruntatum, avarukkut teriyāta mutunūlkaḷi avar māṇākkarāṉa ṭākṭar u.vē. cāminātiyaravarkaḷ kaṇṭatumāṉa nilikku oppiṭalām.
tolkāppiyar akattiyarkkut teriyāta muntunūl kaṇṭamipaṟṟiyē, tam nūlil akattiyattippaṟṟi eṅkēṉum kuṟippiṭavilli yeṉka. tolkāppiyam akattiyattiṟkup pintiṉatātaliṉ, atiṉum ciṟappāy ilakkaṇa meḻutappaṭṭatu. akattiyam vaḻakkaṟṟamikku ituvum oru kāraṇamākum. itiyaṟiyātār piṟkālattil, tolkāppiyar akattiyattic cāvittatāka oru kati kaṭṭiṉar.
tolkāppiyar akattiyarit tam āciriyarākavēṉum ōriṭattum kuṟikkāmiyāl, av viruvarkkumirunta toṭarpu, vētanāyakam piḷḷikkum mīṉāṭcicuntaram piḷḷikku miruntatu pōṉṟatō eṉṟu aiyuṟak kiṭakkiṉṟatu.
akattiyar tamiḻnūṟ payiṟciyip putuppittami
ṭākṭar u.vē. cāminātiyar avarkaḷ, eṅṅaṉam maṭaṅkaḷiṉum tamiḻppulavar vīṭukaḷiṉum payilappaṭātu kuvintu kiṭanta pala tamiḻ nūlkaḷit tiraṭṭi acciṭṭut tamiḻulakukkut tantārkaḷō, aṅṅaṉamē akattiyarum ciṟitupōḻtu payilappaṭātu kiṭanta tamiḻnūlkaḷit tiraṭ ṭiyārāyntu, tamiḻnūṟ kalviyip putuppittāreṉka.
itaṉāl, talikkaḻakam muṭintapiṉṉarē akattiyar teṉṉāṭṭukku vantāreṉpatum, ak kaḻakam niṉṟupōṉa mikkuc cila āriyarē kāraṇamā yiruntirukkalāmeṉpatum, iṅṅaṉam tamiḻit taḷarttōrum tamiḻi vaḷarttōrumāka irucārār tamiḻnāṭṭiṟku vanta āriyar eṉpatum nuṇittaṟiyap paṭum.
mēṉāṭṭil mutaṉmutal ilakkaṇam varintavar piḷāṟṟō (Plato, B.C. 427) eṉṟum, avar peyarccollum viṉiccollumē kaṇṭupiṭittār eṉṟum ataṉpiṉ avartam māṇākkarāṉa arisṟṟāṭṭil (Aristotle, B.C. 384) iṭiccollum eccamuṅ kaṇṭupiṭittāreṉṟum, mēṉāṭ
ṭilakkaṇaṅkaṭkellām aṭippaṭiyāṉatum viḷakkamāṉatum uṇmiyil ilakkaṇameṉṟu collattakkatum, ṭiyōṉi ciyas tirāks (Dionysius Thrax, B.C. 100) eḻutiya ilakkaṇamē yeṉṟum mākkacu mullar kūṟukiṟār.
vaṭamoḻiyil niṟivāṉa ilakkaṇamākiya pāṇiṉīyam ki.mu. 5ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉatu. ataṟku muṉṉamē pala ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyi liruntaṉa. āyiṉum, avi yāvum vaṭamoḻi mutaṟ pāviyamāṉa vāṉmīki yirāmāyaṇattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭaviyē. vaṭamoḻiyilakkaṇaṅkaḷ mutaṉmutal āriya maṟikkē eḻuntaṉavēṉum, avi tolkāppiyattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭavi eṉpataṟku eḷḷaḷavum aiyamilli. avi āriyar tamiḻilakkaṇatti yaṟintapiṉṉarē iyaṟṟappaṭṭaviyeṉpatu piṉṉar viḷakkappaṭum.
iṅṅaṉam, ulakattilēyē mutaṉmutal tiruntiyatum ilakkaṇameḻutappeṟṟatumāṉa tamiḻ, itupōtu oru puṉmoḻiyiṉum iḻivā yeṇṇappaṭuvataṟkuk kāraṇam, iṟṟit tamiḻariṉ aṟiyāmiyum maṭimiyumēyaṉṟi vēṟaṉṟu.
āriyar tam maṟinūlkaḷit tamiḻarkku neṭuṅkālam maṟittuvittatu, avaṟṟiṉ tāḻvu veḷiyākāmip poruṭṭēyaṉṟi, avaṟṟiṉ tūymiyik kāttaṟ poruṭ ṭaṉṟu. tamiḻiluḷḷa kalinūlkaḷi moḻipeyarttum, avaṟṟi virivākkiyum, vaṭamoḻiyilakkiyatti mika vaḷarttukkoṇṭa piṉpuṅkūṭa, avar tam maṟinūlkaḷit tamiḻarkku nērē
yaṟivikkavē yilli. mēṉāṭṭārē mutaṉmutal avaṟṟik kaṟṟut tam moḻikaḷiṟ peyarttut tamiḻarkkaṟivittaṉar. ippōtu uṇmi veḷiyākiviṭṭatēyeṉṟu, āriyar tam muṉṉōr ki.mu. 2500 āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ iyaṟṟiya eḷiya maṟimoḻikaṭku, iv virupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkuriya viḻumiya karuttukaḷi yellām poruttiyurikkiṉṟaṉar. itaṉ poruntāmi ārāycciyillārkkup pulaṉākātupōyiṉum, aḵtuḷḷārkkup pōkāteṉka.
vaṭatici uyarntum teṉtici tāḻntatum
akattiyar teṟkē vantapiṉ, nilam vaṭaticiyil uyarntatum teṉticiyil tāḻntatum muṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭatu. iṅṅaṉamē, kalvi, kittoḻil, vāṇikam, celvam, aluval, atikāram mutaliya piṟavaṟṟilum avviru ticikaḷum (āriyamum tirāviṭamum) muṟiyē uyarntum tāḻntum pōyiṉaveṉ ṟaṟintukoḷka.
mēṟku kiḻakku eṉṉum ticip peyarkaḷ pōṉṟē, uttaram (vaṭakku) takkaṇam (teṟku) eṉṉum ticip peyarkaḷum ēṟṟa viṟakkaṅkaḷi yuṇarttuvatu piṉṉark kūṟappaṭum. 
kaṉṉaṭattil ilakkiyam tōṉṟiyatu ki.pi. 8ām nūṟṟāṇṭeṉṟum, teluṅkil tōṉṟiyatu ki.pi. 10ām nūṟṟāṇṭeṉṟum, maliyāḷattil tōṉṟiyatu ki.pi. 13ām nūṟṟāṇṭeṉṟum collappaṭukiṉṟatu. tamiḻilakkiyam tōṉṟiya kālam iṉṉa nūṟṟāṇṭeṉṟu variyaṟuttuk kūṟa iyalātavāṟu, attuṇip paḻimiyāyiruttaliṉ, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil tamiḻē moḻiyāyiṉum nūlāyiṉum - mikat toṉmi vāyntateṉpatic colla vēṇṭuvatēyilli.
tamiḻiṉ tiruttam
tirāviṭa moḻikaṭkuḷ tamiḻ mikat tiruntiyateṉpatu, ataṉ col vaṭivaṅkaḷālum ilakkaṇac ciṟappālum aṟiyappaṭum.
tamiḻccol vaḷam
(1) tamiḻiṉ vaḷam
tamiḻiṉ colvaḷattit tamiḻarē iṉṉum cariyāy uṇarntilar.
uṇmiyuṭimi yeṉṉuṅ kuṇam, niṉivu col ceyal eṉṟa mukkaraṇaṅkaḷiyun taḻuviyatu eṉpati uṇarttaṟku, uṇmi (uḷ+mi), vāymi (vāy+mi), meymmi (mey+mi) eṉa mūṉṟucoṟkaḷ tamiḻiluḷḷaṉa.
maṉattiṉ vevvēṟu viṉiyiyuṅ kuṟittaṟku vev vēṟu col uḷatu.
kā : uḷ(ḷu), to will (uḷḷam - will); uṇar, to feel, to 
comprehend (uṇarvu - sentiment, uṇarcci - feeling); niṉi, to think, to remember (niṉivu - thought, niṉippu - memory); niṉivukūr, to remember, to commemorate; K‹(D), to propose, to think, to intend (muṉṉam  - intention, indication); muṉṉiṭu, to propose, to set before; muṉṉiṭṭu, having proposed, having set before, for the purpose of. oru kāriyatti muṉṉiṭṭu eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka. muṉṉīṭu = proposal; uṉ(ṉu), to imagine (uṉṉam - imagination); uṉṉi, to guess (uṉṉippu - guessing); eṇ(ṇu), to deliberate, to consider (eṇṇam - consideration); karutu, to conceive, to think (karuttu - concept, idea); aṟi, to know (aṟivu - knowledge); koḷ, to opine (kōḷ - opinion, koḷki - doctrine); mati, to estimate, to regard (matippu - estimation, approximation, respect, mati - sense); tīrmāṉi, to determine, to resolve; ea, to appreciate (nayappu - appreciation); teruḷ, to perceive clearly; maruḷ, to be deluded; āy, to test, to examine, (āyvu - test); ārāy, to make a critical study, to investigate (ārāycci - research, critical study); cūḻ, to counsel, to deliberate, cūḻavi - council.
uḷattoḻilpaṟṟi iṉṉum pala coṟkaḷuḷa. ivaṟṟoṭu tuṇiviṉi cērttukkoḷḷiṉ, ikkālattiya maṉattoḻil nuṭpavēṟupāṭupaṟṟiya karuttukaḷi yellām kuṟikkac coṟkaḷi yamittukkoḷḷalām.
maṉivikkuk kaṇavaṉōṭuḷḷa pulaviyiṉ mūṉṟu nilikaḷiyuṅ kuṟittaṟku, muṟiyē ūṭal, pulavi, tuṉi eṉa mūṉṟu coṟkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.
oru viṉikkut takunta camiyam, nalla karuttil cevvi eṉṟum tīya karuttil aṟṟam eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
tamiḻiṉ colvaḷatti naṉṟāyuṇartaṟku nilittiṇic coṟkaḷi nōkkavēṇṭum.
mutalāvatu uḷḷīṭuḷḷa nilittiṇi yuyirikaḷi marameṉṟum, aḵtillātavaṟṟip pulleṉṟum iruvakiyāka vakuttaṉar muṉṉōr.
puṟakkā ḻaṉavē pulleṉa moḻipa	(marapu. 81)
akakkā ḻaṉavē marameṉa moḻipa	(marapu. 21)
eṉpaṉa tolkāppiyam.
nilittiṇi yuṟuppukaḷil ovvoṉṟiṟkum ataṉataṉ nuṭpa vēṟupāṭṭiṟkēṟpa vevvēṟu peyaruḷatu.
iliyeṉṉum orēyuṟuppiṟku nālvēṟu coṟkaḷuḷa. mā vāḻi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉatu ili yeṉṟum, nel kēḻvaraku mutaliyavaṟṟiṉatu tāḷ eṉṟum, karumpu, peruñcōḷam mutaliyavaṟṟiṉatu tōkiyeṉṟum, teṉṉi paṉi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉatu ōliyeṉṟuṅ kūṟappaṭum.
pū mutalāvatu tōṉṟumpōtu arumpu eṉṟum, pērarumpāṉapōtu pōtu eṉṟum, malarntapiṉ malar eṉṟum, viḻuntapiṉ vī eṉṟum, vāṭiyapiṉ cemmal eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. pū eṉpatu potuppeyar.
arumpu eṉṉum oru nilikkē, ataṉataṉ aḷavukkut takkapaṭi, arumpu, moṭṭu, mukiḻ, muki, mokkuḷ eṉa vevvēṟu coṟkaḷuḷa.
pūkkaḷ kōṭṭuppū, koṭippū, nīrppū, nilappū eṉa nālvakiyāka vakukkappaṭum. ivaṟṟuṭaṉ, ceṭippū eṉpa toṉṟum cērttukkoḷḷalām.
kāyiṉ vevvēṟu nilikaḷ pūmpiñcu, tirukupiñcu, iḷampiñcu, piñcu, arikkāy, kāy, mukkāṟkāy, kaṉṉaṟkāy allatu paḻakkāy, kaṭukkāy allatu karukkāy eṉa vevvēṟu coṟkaḷāṟ kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
māmpiñcu vaṭu eṉṟum, palāppiñcu mūcu eṉṟum vitappittuk kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
paruvattiṟ kāyppatu paruvakkāy eṉṟum, paruvamallāta kālattiṟ kāyppatu vampakkāy eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
mutirntapiṉ kaṉivillātatu kāy eṉṟum, kaṭiṉamāṉatu neṟṟu eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
cevviyāyp paḻukkāta paḻaṅkaḷ, vevvēṟu kāraṇam paṟṟic civiyal, cūmpal, vempal, cotti eṉap palavaki yākak kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ, cottivaki coṇṭu, cotti, coṭṭi eṉap pala peyarāṟ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. cottiyāṉa miḷakup paḻam allatu vaṟṟal coṇṭu eṉṉuñ collāl maṭṭum kuṟikkappaṭutal kāṇka.
paḻattiṉ tōlvakikaṭku, ataṉataṉ vaṉmi meṉmik kut takkapaṭi toli, tōl, tōṭu, ōṭu, ciraṭṭi eṉap pala 
peyarkaḷuḷa.
vitivakikku vittu, viti, maṇi, muttu, koṭṭi eṉa vevvēṟu coṟkaḷuḷa.
iṅṅaṉam, ovvōr uṟuppiṟkum paṟpala coṟkaḷuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟiyellām eṉatu centamiḻc coṟpiṟappiyal akaramutaliyiṟ kaṇṭukoḷka.
nelvaki maṭṭum nūṟṟukkaṇakkā yuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ campā eṉṉum oruvakiyē 70 tiṟamāyuḷḷatu.
āṅkilam ikkālattil palvēṟu moḻikaḷiṉiṉṟum ērāḷamāṉa coṟkaḷik kaṭaṉkoṇṭiruppatāl, ekkarutti yum terivikkavalla moḻiyākap pukaḻappaṭukiṉṟatu. āṉāl, iv virupatām nūṟṟāṇṭilum, āṅkilattilum atupōṉṟa piṟa perumoḻikaḷilum illāta pala coṟkaḷ ki.mu. 3000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻiliruntatu mikamika viyakkattakkatē.
tamiḻil oruporuṭ coṟkaḷ palaporuḷkaṭkuḷḷaṉa. piṟa tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ, pala poruḷkaṭkut tamiḻccoṟkaḷiyē ovvoṉṟum ovvoṉṟākat terintukoṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.
kā : il - illu (teluṅku). maṉi - maṉa (kaṉṉaṭam).
(2) tamiḻttāymi
tirāviṭac coṟkaṭku mūlam perumpālum tamiḻilēyē uḷḷatu.
kā : veṉṉa (te.) < veṇṇey = veḷ + ney.
	     lētu (te.) < ilatu.
cey (ki) eṉpatu pōṉṟa iraṇṭoru coṟkaḷ, mūlavaṭivil teluṅkiliruppatu, potuvitikkut tavircciyē yaṉṟi māṟākāteṉka.
(3) tamiḻttūymi
tamiḻip pōlap piṟa tirāviṭa moḻikaḷip piṟa moḻik kalappiṉṟi yeḻuta muṭiyātu.
teluṅkil uṭalukku vaḻaṅkum oḷḷu, tēkam eṉṉum irucoṟkaḷil, oḷḷu eṉpatu uṭal eṉṉum tamiḻccolliṉ citivu; tēkam eṉpatu vaṭacol.
(4) tamiḻiṉ muṉmi
piṟa tirāviṭa moḻiccoṟkaḷ mūlattiṟkut tamiḻic cārntiruppatupōla, tamiḻccoṟkaḷ maṟittum tam mūlattiṟkup piṟamoḻiyic cārntiruppaviyalla.
(5) tamiḻal tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ ticivaḻakkuttaṉmi
tamiḻallāta tirāviṭa moḻikaḷellām, āriyar intiyāviṟku varumuṉ, tamiḻiṉ kiḷivaḻakkukaḷākavē yiruntaṉa.
tamiḻiluḷḷa coṟkaḷ, iyaṟcol tiricol ticic col vaṭacol eṉṟu nālvakiyākat tolkāppiyattil vakukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, mutal mūṉṟum taṉcol; iṟuti yoṉṟum ayaṟcol.
mutal mūṉṟaṉuḷ, muṉṉiraṇṭum centamiḻccol; piṉṉoṉṟum koṭuntamiḻccol.
centamiḻnāṭu
centamiḻ nilatti vikiyāṟṟiṉ vaṭakkum marutayāṟ ṟiṉ teṟkum karuvūriṉ kiḻakkum maruvūriṉ mēṟkumām eṉṟu iḷampūraṇar, cēṉāvariyar, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar mutaliyōr urittaṉar .
centamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ ciṟanta pakuti iṉṟum pāṇṭi nāṭēyā yiruttaliṉ, iv vuri poruntātu.
centamiḻ nāṭāvatu: viyiyāṟṟiṉ vaṭakkum, maruta yāṟṟiṉ teṟkum, karuvūriṉ kiḻakkum, maruvūriṉ mēṟkum eṉpa. ivvāṟu urittaṟku ōr ilakkaṇaṅ kāṇāmiyāṉum, viyi yāṟṟiṉ teṟkākiya koṟkiyum, karuvūriṉ mēṟkākiya koṭuṅkōḷūrum, marutayāṟṟiṉ vaṭakkākiya kāñciyum tamiḻtiri nilamātal vēṇṭumātalāṉum, aḵtu uriyaṉṟu eṉpār urikkumāṟu :
vaṭavēṅkaṭan teṉkumari
 āyiṭit
 tamiḻkūṟu nallulakattu 
 vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷu māyiru mutaliṉ
 eḻuttuñ collum poruḷum nāṭi
eṉṟamiyāṉum, itaṉuḷ tamiḻ kūṟum nallulakameṉa vicēṭit tamiyāṉum, kiḻakkum mēṟkum elli kūṟātu teṟkelli kūṟiyavataṉāṟ kumariyiṉ teṟkākiya nāṭukaḷiyoḻittu, vēṅkaṭamaliyiṉ teṟkum, kumariyiṉ vaṭakkum, kuṇakaṭaliṉ mēṟkum, kuṭakaṭaliṉ kiḻakkumākiya nilam centamiḻ nilameṉ ṟurippa eṉṟurittār teyvac ciliyār.
iv vuriyē ciṟantatākum. centamiḻ nilam,
cantaṉap potiyac centamiḻ muṉiyuñ
 cavuntara pāṇṭiya ṉeṉuntamiḻ nāṭaṉuñ
 caṅkap pulavarum taḻittiṉi tirukkum
 maṅkalap pāṇṭi vaḷanā ṭeṉpa
eṉpatu piṟkālattiṟ kēṟṟatākum.
centamiḻ cērnta paṉṉiru nilattum
 taṅkuṟip piṉavē ticiccoṟ kiḷavi	(tol. ecca. 4)
eṉpatāl, centamiḻnāṭ ṭelli tāṇṭiya paṉṉiru nāṭukaḷ koṭuntamiḻnāṭu eṉpatum, avaṟṟuḷ vitappāy vaḻaṅkiya 
coṟkaḷ ticiccoṟkaḷ eṉpatum peṟappaṭum.
koṭuntamiḻnāṭu
koṭuntamiḻnāṭu paṉṉiraṇṭiyum,
teṉpāṇṭi kuṭṭaṅ kuṭaṅkaṟkā vēṇpūḻi
 paṉṟi yaruvā vataṉvaṭakku - naṉṟāya
 cīta malāṭu puṉaṉāṭu centamiḻcēr
 ētamil paṉṉirunāṭ ṭeṇ
eṉṉum veṇpāviṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉavākak kūṟuvar pala uriyāciriyar. iviyellām mutaṟkālattil centamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ pakutikaḷākak koḷḷappaṭṭamiyiṉ, iv vuri poruntātu. in nāṭukaḷuḷ vēṇāṭu puṉaṉāṭu eṉṟa iraṇṭiṟkup patilāka, poṅkarnāṭu oḷināṭu eṉpavaṟṟik kuṟippar iḷampūraṇar, cēṉāvariyar mutaliyōr. poṅkar nāṭṭip potuṅkar nāṭeṉpar iḷampūraṇar. 
tāyit taḷḷi eṉpa kuṭanāṭṭār. nāyi ñamali eṉpa pūḻināṭṭār eṉṟu ticiccoṟkuk kāṭṭuk kūṟiṉar iḷampūraṇar. iṅkuk kuṟikkappaṭṭa irunāṭukaḷum ippōtu maliyāḷa nāṭṭiṉ pakutikaḷāka vuḷḷaṉa.
nacciṉārkkiṉiyar piṉvarumāṟu ticiccoṟkuk kāṭṭuk kūṟiṉar:
teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭār ā erumi eṉpaṉavaṟṟip peṟṟam eṉṟum; kuṭanāṭṭār tāyit taḷḷi eṉṟum, nāyi ñeḷḷi eṉṟum; kuṭṭanāṭṭār tantiyi accaṉ eṉṟum; kaṟkānāṭṭār vañcarik kiyar eṉṟum; cītanāṭṭār ēṭāveṉpataṉi eluvaṉ eṉṟum, tōḻiyi ikuḷi eṉṟum, tammāmiyeṉpataṉit tantuvi eṉṟum; pūḻināṭṭār nāyi ñamali eṉṟum, ciṟukuḷattip pāḻi eṉṟum; aruvānāṭṭār ceyyic ceṟu eṉṟum, ciṟukuḷattik kēṇi eṉṟum; aruvā vaṭataliyār kuṟuṇiyik kuṭṭi eṉṟum vaḻaṅkupa.
iṉic ciṅkaḷam antōveṉpatu; karunaṭaṅ kariya cikka kuḷira eṉpaṉa; vaṭuku ceppeṉpatu; teluṅku eruttip pāṇṭileṉpatu; tuḷu māmarattik kokkeṉpatu. oḻintavaṟṟiṟkum vantuḻik kāṇka.
itaṉāl teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam mutaliyavi orukālattil tamiḻiṉ ticivaḻakkukaḷākavē yiruntaṉa veṉṟum, piṉpu kiḷi vaḻakkukaḷāki, iṟutiyil vaṭacoṟ kalappāl vēṟu moḻikaḷāyp pirintuviṭṭaṉa veṉṟum aṟiyappaṭum.
teyvacciliyārum ik karutti yoṭṭiyē,
centamiḻ nāṭṭic cērnta paṉṉiru nilattu muḷḷōr tattaṅ kuṟippiṉiyuṭiya ticic collākiya col eṉṟavāṟu.
paṉṉiru nilamāvaṉa : viyiyāṟṟiṉ... nāṭu, oḷināṭu, teṉpāṇṭināṭu, karuṅkuṭṭanāṭu, kuṭanāṭu, paṉṟināṭu, kaṟkānāṭu, cītanāṭu, pūḻināṭu, malāṭu, aruvānāṭu, aruvā vaṭatali eṉpa. ivi centamiḻ nāṭṭakatta. centamiḻ (cērnta) nāṭeṉṟamiyāl, piṟanāṭākal vēṇṭumeṉpār eṭuttuk kāṭṭumāṟu : kaṉṉit teṉkarik kaṭaṟ paḻantīpam kollam kūpakam ciṅkaḷa meṉṉum elliyiṉ puṟattavum, kaṉṉaṭam vaṭukam kaliṅkam teliṅkam koṅkaṇam tuḷuvam kuṭakam kuṉṟakam eṉpaṉa kuṭapāliru puṟacciyattuṭa ṉuṟipukūṟun tamiḻtiri nilaṅkaḷum, muṭi yuṭiyavariṭu nilavāṭciyiṉ aracu mēmpaṭṭa kuṟunilak kuṭumikaḷ patiṉmarum uṭaṉiruppiruvaruvākiya paṉṉiruvar aracarum paṭitta paṉṉiru tēyattiṉum, tamiḻc collātaṟku viruppuṭiyaṉa eṉṟamiyāṉum, tamiḻkūṟum nallulakattu...... poruḷum nāṭi eṉa niṟuttup piṉṉuñ centamiḻiyaṟki civaṇiya nilattoṭu muntunūl kaṇṭu eṉa ōtiyavataṉāṟ civaṇiya nilamāvatu elli kuṟitta nilattic cārnta nilameṉa vēṇṭutalāṉum, paṉṉiru nilamāvaṉa :
kumariyāṟṟiṉ teṉkarippaṭṭa paḻantīpamum kollamum kūpakamum ciṅkaḷamum, ciyattiṉ mēṟkuppaṭṭa koṅkaṇamum tuḷuvamum kuṭakamum kuṉṟakamum, kiḻakkuppaṭṭa karunaṭamum vaṭukum teliṅkum kaliṅkamum eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭum.
ivaṟṟuḷ, kūpakamum kollamum kaṭalkoḷḷap paṭutaliṉ, kumariyāṟṟiṉ vaṭakariyik kollameṉak kuṭiyēṟiṉār pōlum. pañcattirāviṭameṉavum vaṭanāṭṭār urippavākalāṉ, aviyintum vēṅkaṭattiṉ teṟkātaluṅ kūṭāmi yuṇarka.
annilattu vaḻaṅkuñ collākic ceñcolliṉ vēṟupaṭṭuc cāṉṟōr ceyyuḷakattu varuvaṉa nīkkappaṭā eṉṟavāṟu.
kuṭāvaṭiyuḷiyam eṉṟavaḻi, kuṭāvaṭi eṉpatu kuṭakattār piḷḷikaṭku iṭṭa peyar. antō eṉpatu ciṅkaḷavar aiyō eṉpataṟ kiṭṭa peyar. yāṉ taṟkariya varutu eṉṟavaḻi karital eṉpatu karunāṭar viḷipporuḷuṇarak kūṟuvatu. ceppu eṉpatu vaṭukar collutaṟkup peyarāka vaḻaṅkuvatu. pāṇṭil eṉpatu teluṅkar pacuviṟkum eruttiṟkum peyarāka vaḻaṅkuvatu. kokku eṉpatu tuḷuvar māvukkup peyarāka vaḻaṅkuvatu. piṟavum ivvāṟu varuvaṉa palavaṟṟiyum vanta vaḻik kaṇṭukoḷka eṉṟu kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka.2
teluṅku kaṉṉaṭa mutaliyavi piṟkālattil piṟamoḻi kaḷāyp pirintupōṉapiṉpu, avaṟṟiṉ muṉṉi niliyi
yuṇarātu avi vaḻaṅkum nāṭukaḷiyum ayaṉmoḻi nāṭu kaḷiyuñ cērttuk koṭuntamiḻnāṭākak kūṟiṉar pavaṇantiyār,

teluṅku kaṉṉaṭam mutaliyavi tamiḻiṉiṉṟum pirintu pōṉapiṉpē, poṅkarnāṭu oḷināṭu mutaliya paḻañcentamiḻ

nāṭṭup pakutikaḷ koṭuntamiḻnāṭākak kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. itaṉāl, muṉpu koṭuntamiḻ nāṭāyiruntavi pala piṉpu piṟamoḻi nāṭāy māṟiviṭṭaṉa eṉpatum, centamiḻ nāṭu varavarat teṟku nōkki oṭuṅkikkoṇṭē varukiṉṟa teṉpatum, itaṟkuk kāraṇam āriyak kalappum centamiḻt toṭarpiṉmiyum eṉpatum aṟiyappaṭum.
ceppu eṉpatu, collutal eṉṉum poruḷilmaṭṭum ticiccollākum. maṟṟappaṭi atu centamiḻc collē eṉpatu,
ceppum viṉāvum vaḻāal ōmpal	(kiḷavi. 13)
aḵtaṉ ṟeṉpa veṇpā yāppē	(cey. 78)
eṉpavaṟṟāl aṟiyappaṭum.
ceppu eṉpatu, teluṅkil collutaliyum, tamiḻil viṭi collutaliyuṅ kuṟittal kāṇka. atōṭu tamiḻilakkaṇan tōṉṟiya toṉmutu kālattilēyē. ceppu eṉpatu ōr ilakkaṇak kuṟiyīṭāyamintatiyum nōkkuka. (ceppal veṇpā vōci.)
ī tā koṭu eṉṉum mūṉṟu centamiḻc coṟkaḷil, ī eṉpatu eṅṅaṉam taṉ nuṇporuḷi yiḻantu teluṅkil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatō, aṅṅaṉamē ceppu eṉpatum eṉka.
tolkāppiyar kālattil, tamiḻil vaḻaṅkiya ayaṉmoḻic col vaṭacol oṉṟē. ataṉālēyē atu taṉ peyarāl vaṭa col eṉappaṭṭatu. ataṉpaṭi ippōtu tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum ayaṉmoḻic coṟkaḷiyellām, āṅkilac col, intus tāṉic col eṉa avvam moḻippeyarālēyē kūṟal vēṇṭum. tirāviṭamoḻic coṟkaḷ maṭṭum ticiccollā kavē kūṟappaṭum.
tolkāppiyar kālattil tamiḻil vaḻaṅkiṉa vaṭacoṟ kaḷum, arukiya vaḻakkēyaṉṟip perukiya vaḻakkaṉṟu. av varukiya vaḻakkum vēṇṭāmiyāyt tamiḻik keṭuttaṟkeṉṟē pukuttappaṭṭatākum.
vaṭacoṟ kiḷavi vaṭaveḻut torīi
 eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta collā kummē
eṉṟu tolkāppiyar kūṟiyatu, vaṭacol mēṉmēluṅ kalantu tamiḻt tūymiyik keṭukkātavāṟu oruvāṟu taṭi ceytatē yaṉṟi, ikkālattuc cilar eṇṇuvatu pōla vaṭacolliyum piṟacolliyum tārāḷamāyc cērttukkoḷḷumāṟu viṭi tantataṉṟu.
(8) tirāviṭam eṉṉuñ coṉmūlam
paḻiya kālattil nāṭṭup peyarkaḷum moḻippeyar kaḷum perumpālum am īṟu peṟṟut tamiḻil vaḻaṅkiṉa.
kā : īḻam, kaṭāram, cīṉam, yavaṉam.
tamiḻam - tramiḷa(m) - tramiṭa(m) - traviṭa(m) - trāviṭa(m) eṉṉum muṟiyil, tamiḻam eṉṉum collē tirāviṭam eṉṟu tirintatākum.
tamiḻam eṉpatu tamiḷa - taviḷa - taviṭa eṉṟu pirākirutattil tirintapiṉpu, tamiḷa taviṭa eṉṉum vaṭivaṅkaḷ tramila, tiramiṭa, traviṭa eṉṟu vaṭamoḻiyil tirintatāka, paṇṭitar kiriyarcaṉ (Dr. Grierson) kūṟuvar.3 eṅṅaṉamiruppiṉum, tamiḻam eṉṉum collē traviṭa eṉṟu tirintateṉpataṟku eṭṭuṇiyum aiyamilli.
kālṭuvel aiyar itupaṟṟit talikīḻākak kūṟiṉar. avar tavaṟṟik kiriyarcaṉuṅ kuṟittuḷḷār.
tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ ellāvaṟṟiṟkum, tamiḻam eṉṉum peyarē potuppeyarāka mutalāvatu vaḻaṅki vantatu. trāviṭam eṉṉum vaṭivum, tamiḻam eṉṉum poruḷilēyē, mutaṉ mutal vaḻaṅkiyatākum. teluṅku tamiḻiṉiṉṟum pirintupōṉa piṉpu, muṉpu oṉṟāyeṇṇappaṭṭa tirāviṭa moḻi iraṇṭāyk karutappaṭṭu, āntira tirāviṭa pāṣā eṉṉum iṇippeyarāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭatu. ataṉpiṉ, kaṉṉaṭam, maliyāḷam mutaliya
3	Linguistic Survey of India, Vol. IV, p. 298
moḻikaḷ pirintu tirāviṭam palkiyapiṉ, tamiḻam eṉṉum peyariṉ maṟuvaṭivamāṉa tamiḻ tirāviṭam eṉṉum coṟkaḷuḷ, muṉṉatu tamiḻmoḻikkum piṉṉatu tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ ellāvaṟṟukkumāka variyaṟukkappaṭṭaṉa.
itukāṟum kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, tamiḻē tirāviṭa moḻikaṭkuḷ mikat toṉmiyāṉatum mikat tiruntiyatum, mika vaḷamuḷḷatum, ataṉāl mikacciṟanta tirāviṭa eccamāṉatum eṉṟaṟintu koḷka.4
1. tirāviṭam vaṭakkunōkkit tirital
(i) tamiḻnāṭṭil, pāṇṭināṭṭup pakutiyākiya teṉpākamē iṉṟum tamiḻukkuc ciṟantatāka vuḷatu.
pāṇṭiyaṉukkut tamiḻnāṭaṉ eṉṟa peyar tivākarattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ākavē, pāṇṭināṭu tamiḻnāṭeṉac ciṟappikkappaṭṭatākum.
cōḻanāṭṭit tamiḻnāṭeṉṟum, toṇṭināṭṭic cāṉṟōruṭittu eṉṟum kūṟiyatu piṟkālamākum. tamiḻaracark kuḷ, mutalāvatu āriyattiṟku muṟṟum aṭimippaṭṭavaṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ. ataṉālēyē, iṉṟu teṉpākattil vaṭapākat tiliruppatupōṉṟa tamiḻuṇarcci yilli. āyiṉum, nāṭṭup puṟattiluḷḷa kuṭiyāṉavarkaḷiṭip paṇṭit tamiḻccoṟ kaḷum vaḻakkukaḷum pala iṉṟum aḻiyātiruntu, pāṇṭi nāṭṭiṉ paḻam perumiyi oru ciṟitu kāttukkoṇ ṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.
2. tamiḻnāṭṭil teṉpākam ciṟantateṉpataṟkuk kāraṇaṅkaḷ
(1)	tamiḻi vaḷartta allatu kātta mūṉṟu kaḻakaṅkaḷum pāṇṭināṭṭi liruntami.
(2)	vaṭapākattil vaḻaṅkāta pala coṟkaḷum paḻa moḻikaḷum vaḻaṅkal.
(3)	vaṭacoṟkaḷ taṟpavamāka vaḻaṅkal.
	kā : cākṣi - cākki.

	cuttam, mirācutār mutaliya coṟkaḷukkut tuppuravu, paṇṇiyār mutaliya taṉittamiḻc coṟkaḷ vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
(4)	coṟkaḷ uṇmi vaṭivil vaḻaṅkal.
	kā : irāmam - (nāmam).

	kā : bicycle - mitivaṇṭi.
(6)	vaṭapākattilillāta pala payirkaḷ ceyyappaṭal.
	kā : kāṭikkaṇṇi, cāmi, kutirivāli.

vaṭavēṅkaṭam eṉṟu, vēṅkaṭam tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ vaṭavelliyākat tolkāppiyap pāyirattiṟ kūṟap paṭṭuḷḷatu. āyiṉum, tamiḻkūṟum nallulakattu eṉṟataṉāl, atic centamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ vaṭavelliyākak koḷvatē poruttamākum.
vēṅkaṭam ippōtu teluṅka nāṭṭiṟku uṭpaṭṭu viṭṭatu.
teluṅku mikutiyum āriyattaṉmiyaṭintu, āriyattiṟkum tirāviṭattiṟkum iṭippaṭṭa niliyiluḷḷatu.
teluṅkil eḻuttolikaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiṟpōl vallōci peṟukiṉṟaṉa; vakaram pakaramākavum ḻakaram ṭakaramākavum māṟukiṉṟaṉa; vaṭacoṟkaḷ mikat tārāḷamāy vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. oru ceyyuḷ perumpālum vaṭacoṟkaḷā yiruntāl, mikavuyarnta teluṅkāka matikkappaṭukiṉṟatu.
teluṅku vaṭivākap pala teṉcoṟkaḷ mēliyāriya moḻikaḷilum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.

kēḷ eṉṉum col, viṉavu eṉṉum poruḷil tamiḻil vaḻaṅkuvatupōla, aṭuku eṉṉum col teluṅkil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟa teṉka. Write eṉṉum collil (v) paṇṭu olittatu.
(v) teluṅkiṟku vaṭakkil āriyamoḻi vaḻaṅkal
paṇṭik kālattil, tamiḻ, teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam, marāṭṭi, kujarātti eṉṉum aintiyum, āriyarē pañca trāviṭī eṉṟu aḻittaṉar. kālṭuvel aiyar ittokuppi oppuk koḷḷavilli. āyiṉum, ittokuppu cariyāṉatē yeṉpatu, immaṭalattiṉ 2ām pākattil viḷakkappaṭum.
(vi) vaṭa intiyāvil tirāviṭa maṟivu
kōṇṭi (Gondi), patrī (Bhatri), mālṟṟō (Malto), pōy (Bhoi) mutaliya tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ, meḷḷa meḷḷa āriya mayamāvati allatu maṟintupōvatip paṇṭitar kiriyarcaṉ 1906ām āṇṭē tamatu intiya moḻik kaṇakkīṭu eṉṉum nūliṟ kuṟittuḷḷār.5
5	L.S.I. Vol. IV, pp.446, 472-4
(vii) vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷil tirāviṭa aṭiyāḷam
 āriyar varumuṉ vaṭa intiyāvil vaḻaṅkiya tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ, avar vantapiṉ āriyattoṭu kalantupōṉami yāl, āriyattiṟku māṟāṉa pala tirāviṭa amitikaḷ iṉṟum vaṭanāṭṭu moḻikaḷiluḷḷaṉa. aviyāvaṉa:
(1)	pirikkappaṭum īṟṟurupāl vēṟṟumiyuṇarttal.
(2)	īreṇṇiṟkum vēṟṟumiyuru poṉṟāyiruttal.
(3)	muṉṉiliyi uḷappaṭuttuvatum uḷappaṭuttātatu māṉa iru taṉmippaṉmip peyarkaḷ.
(4)	muṉṉoṭṭukkup patilākap piṉṉoṭṭu vaḻaṅkal.
(5)	viṉiyeccattāṟ kālam amital.
(6)	taḻuvuñ coṟṟoṭar taḻuvappaṭuñ coṟṟoṭarkku muṉṉiṟṟal.
(7)	taḻuvuñcol taḻuvappaṭuñcoṟku K‹Å‰wš.6
intiyil pala tamiḻccoṟkaḷum ilakkaṇa amitikaḷum vaḻakkukaḷum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.

ilakkaṇa amiti
(1) 4ām vēṟṟumiyurupu tamiḻil ku eṉṟum intiyil kō eṉṟu mirukkiṉṟatu.
(2) iru moḻikaḷilum vēṟṟumiyaṭiyuṭaṉ urupu cērntu mūviṭappeyarkaḷ vēṟṟumippaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
kā : eṉmēl, mujmē
(3) tamiḻil iya eṉpatum, intiyil (muṉṉiliyil) iyē eṉpatum viyaṅkōḷ īṟākavuḷḷaṉa.
(4) māṟē7 (māṟu + ē) eṉpatu irumoḻiyilum ētup poru ḷiṭiccollā yuḷḷatu.
intiyil kē eṉṉum urupōṭu cērntē varum.

iṅṅaṉam tamiḻilumuṇṭu. āṉāl, viṉippakutikaḷ iṟantakāla viṉiyeccap poruḷpaṭāmal nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccap poruḷpaṭum.

kā : āyā = vantāṉ, vantu.
	     pōlā = coṉṉāṉ, colli.
ivi ceyyā eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉiyeccamē paṇṭit tamiḻilum viṉimuṟṟāy vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. pāluṇarttum īṟu piṟkālattiṟ cērkkappaṭṭatu.
vaḻakku : pallip piṭuṅkiviṭu eṉṉum vaḻakku ceruk kaṭakku eṉṉum poruḷil, tānt kaṭṭēkartō eṉṟum, uyirik kiyilēntikkoṇṭu ōṭu eṉpatu jāṉlēkkar pāk eṉṟum intiyil vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka.
(viii) intiyāviṟku mēṟkē peluccistāṉa malināṭṭil pirāhuyī eṉṉum tirāviṭac ciṟupuṉmoḻi vaḻaṅkal.
(ix) peluccistāṉattiṟkappāl tirāviṭamoḻi vaḻaṅkāmiyum, tirāviṭac coṟkaḷē vaḻaṅkutalum.
oru moḻi taṉṉāṭṭiṟ cevviyāyiruntu, ayalnāṭu cellaccellat tirivatu iyalpu. āṅkilam iṅkilāntil cevviyākavum, intiyāvil tirintum, āppirikkā cīṉam mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiṟ citintum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉamē tamiḻ allatu tirāviṭamoḻi teṉṉāṭṭiṟ cevviyāyum vaṭakkē cellaccellat tirintum vaḻaṅkuvatiṉāl, tirāviṭariṉ tollakam kumarināṭēyeṉpatu tuṇiyappaṭum.
3. tamiḻakattiṉ toṉmik kuṟippukaḷ
(1) nilattiṉ toṉmi
ñālattil kumarināṭu mikat toṉmiyāṉatu. intiyā viṉ mutaṟ peyar nāvalantīvu eṉpatu. itu nāvalan taṇ poḻil eṉṟum vaḻaṅkum. tamiḻnāṭu muḻuvatum orē poḻil (cōli) pōliruntamiyiṉ, poḻil eṉpatu nāṭu allatu ulakam eṉṟu poruḷpaṭṭatu. itaṉāl tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ makkaṭ perukkaṟṟa oru toṉmutunili yuṇarappaṭum.
carittira kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa tāḻikaḷum aṭakkak kaṟkaḷum ēṉaṅkaḷum, teṉṉāṭṭiṟ palaviṭaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.
mikap paḻimiyāṉa maṇṭiyōṭukaḷuḷ oṉṟu, jāvāviṉiṉṟu vantuḷḷatu. at tīvu taṉṉarukiluḷḷa piṟa tīvukaḷuṭaṉ orukālattil āciya nilattō ṭiṇikkap paṭṭiruntatu. atu kāṭṭumāntaṉ (orang-utan) eṉṉum 
kuraṅku vatiyum iṭaṅkaṭku aṇittāṉatu. māntaṉukkum kuraṅkiṟkum iṭippaṭṭa ōr uyir vatinta iṭamākat teritalāl, atu namakku mika mukkiyamāṉatu8 eṉṟu ālpvreṭ kiḷāṭ (Alfred Clodd) kūṟuvatāl, ataṉōṭu oru kālattil iṇikkappaṭṭirunta kumarināṭṭiṉ toṉmiyum oruvāṟu viḷaṅkum.
yāṉikkiyum maṭaṅkaluṭampum uḷḷa yāḷi eṉṉum vilaṅkum, atupōṉṟa piṟavum paṇṭit tamiḻnāṭṭiliruntatum ataṉ toṉmiyip pulappaṭuttum.
(2) nilattumoḻiyiṉ toṉmi
itu piṉṉark kūṟappaṭum
(3) nilattumakkaḷ vāḻkkimuṟi
nāṭṭuvāḻkkinili: ikkālattiṟpōlac carittira nūlkaḷ muṟkālattil eḻutappaṭāviṭiṉum, mutaṟṟamiḻariṉ vāḻkki muṟi akapporuṭ ceyyuḷkaḷil, ciṟappākak kōviyil, mikak kāvalākap pōṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. makkaḷ evvaḷavu nākarikamaṭiyiṉum, ataṉāl avaratu naṭiyuṭi koḷki evvaḷavu māṟiṉum, paṇṭimuṟipaṟṟiyē eṉṟum pāṭavēṇṭumeṉṟu kōvikku oru marapuḷḷatu. atu 
8	Childhood of the World
pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkam eṉappaṭum. atāvatu, uḷḷatum illatuṅ kalantu iṉiyatākavē yirukkum nāṭaka muṟiyum, iṉiyatum iṉṉātatuṅ kalantu uḷḷatākavē irukkum ulakiyal muṟiyum oruṅkē taḻuviya nūṉeṟi vaḻakkākum.
kuṟiñcināṭṭaracaṉ makaḷukku uṭiyum aṇiyum taḻiyākavē kōviyiṟ kūṟiyiruppatu, mikap paḻaṅkālattu iyalpik kuṟippatākum. ikkālattiṟ karāccip paṭṭaṇiyum oru peṇṇikkuṟittuk kōvi pāṭiṉum, paṇṭit taḻiyē talivaṉ kiyuṟiyākak kūṟappaṭuvataṉṟik karāccippaṭṭu kūṟappaṭātu. iṅṅaṉamē piṟavum. 
paṇṭittamiḻar kuṟiñci, mulli, marutam, neytal, pāli eṉa aintu vakiyāṉa nilattiṟ kuṭiyiruntaṉar. ivi aintiṇi yeṉappaṭum. ivi nilittiṇiyāṟ peyar peṟṟaṉa (iṭavaṉāku peyar). ivaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷuḷ, pāli marutam eṉṉum iraṇṭum marappeyarkaḷ; ēṉiya pūppeyarkaḷ.
pāli eṉpatu piṟanāṭṭiluḷḷatupōṉṟa vaṟaṇṭa maṇal nilamaṉṟu. kuṟiñcikkum mullikkum iṭiyiluḷḷa nilam pālimarac ciṟappāl pāliyeṉappaṭṭatu. atu mutuvēṉiṟ kālattil vaṟaṇṭum maṟṟak kālattil ceḻittumirukkum.
vēṉalaṅ kiḻavaṉoṭu veṅkatir vēntaṉ
 ṟāṉalan tirukat taṉmiyiṟ kuṉṟi
 mulliyuṅ kuṟiñciyu muṟimiyiṟ ṟirintu
 nalliyal piḻantu naṭuṅkutuya ruṟuttup
 pāli yeṉpatōr paṭivaṅ koḷḷum	(cilap. 11 : 12-16)
eṉṟu iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ kūṟutal kāṇka. pāliyiṉ mutuvēṉiṟkāla niliyē, piriviṟkuriyatāka akapporuṭ ceyyuḷkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭum.
makkaḷ aintiṇi nilattiṟkup pirintupōṉatu, kuṟiñci
yiṉiṉṟu pōvatum, ayalnāṭṭiṉiṉṟu vantu kuṭipukuntu pōvatumāka iruvaki. ivaṟṟuḷ, muṉṉatu makkaṭperukkāl paṭippaṭiyāy nikaḻvatu; piṉṉatu terintukōṭalāl orē camiyattum nikaḻakkūṭiyatu. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉatē tamiḻnāṭṭil nikaḻntateṉka.
kuṟiñciyil maṭṭum makkaḷirunta kālamumuṇṭu. atu māntaṉ tōṟṟattiṟku aṭutta niliyākum. tamiḻnūlkaḷ tōṉṟiyatu marutattil nakaram tōṉṟiyapiṉpātaliṉ, kuṟiñciyilmaṭṭum makkaḷirunta toṉṉili appōtu maṟintupōyiṟṟu. ataṉāṟ kuṟikkappaṭavilli. āyiṉum atik karuttaḷaviyāṉ aṟintukoḷḷalām.
māntaṉ tōṟṟam āṇum peṇṇumāyttāṉiruntirukka vēṇṭum. avariyē ātam ēviyeṉṟu kiṟittumatamum islām matamuṅ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
irumutu peruṅkuravariṉiṉṟum pala makkaḷ tōṉṟiya piṉ, kuṟiñciyil iṭampōtāmal, cilar mullikkuc ceṉṟaṉar.
mutaṟkālattil uṇavu tēṭuvatē māntar toḻilā yiruntatu. kuṟiñciyil kāykaṉikaḷip paṟittum vēṭṭiyāṭiyum uṇṭuvanta makkaḷ, iyaṟkiyāy viḷiyum maravuṇavu pōtāmiyālum, vēṇṭiyapōtellām ūṉuṇavu kiṭiyāmiyālum, ceykiyāyp payirpaccikaḷiyum vilaṅkukaḷiyum vaḷarkkattoṭaṅkiṉar. itaṟku maramaṭarnta kuṟiñci vacatiyāyirāmiyāl mullikkuc ceṉṟaṉar. itaṉāl koṭiya vilaṅkukaṭkum ōraḷavu tappiṉar.
māntaṉ mutaṉmutalāy vaḷartta vilaṅku āvē. ā eṉpatu mā eṉpataṉ mey nīkkam. mā eṉṟu kattuvatu māveṉappaṭṭatu. mā-māṉ-māṭu. mā eṉpatu ṉakara meyyīṟu peṟṟu, āviṟkiṉamāṉa māṉi uṇarttiṟṟu. ā eṉpatu māṭu eṉpatupōla mutalāvatu potuppeyarā yiruntu, piṉpu peṇpālukku variyaṟukkappaṭṭatu. mutalāvatu vaḷarkkappaṭṭa vilaṅku mā(ā) vātaliṉ ataṉ peyar vilaṅkiṉattiṟkellām potuppeyarāyiṟṟu. ā eṉpatu ṉakara vīṟupeṟṟu āṉ eṉṟāyiṟṟu.
maravuṇavum ūṉuṇavum makkaṭku vēṇṭiyavāyuḷḷa miyiṉ, uḻavum āvōmpalum oruṅkē tōṉṟiṉaveṉ ṉalām. āvāṉatu pāl tantatuṭaṉ uḻaviṟku vēṇṭiya kaṉṟukaḷiyum īṉṟatu. pulveḷikaḷil māṭukaḷi mēyttuk koṇṭu, ciṟitu puṉceyp payirkaḷiyum viḷittuk koṇṭaṉar mullinilattār.
māṭu paṟiyarālmaṭṭum tiṉṉappaṭukiṉṟatu. mēṉāṭu kaḷil ati ellārum uṇkiṉṟaṉar.
mullinilattiṟ pōtumāṉa nīrvaḷamum nilavaḷamumil lāmiyāl, makkaḷ aṭuttāṟpōl, mīṉip peritum virumpiṉavar neytalnilattiṟkum kūlattip peritum viruppiṉavar marutanilattiṟkumākap pirintupōyiṉar.
pālinilavāṇarkku mutuvēṉilil uṇavu kiṭiyāmi yāl, aṉṟu avar āṟalikkavum cūṟi koḷḷavum nērntatu.
 aintu nilattilum māntar paraviyapiṉ, kuṟiñcinilattār kuṟi colvatāl kuṟavar eṉṟum, pālinilattār pōrt toḻil ceytu maṟam (vīram) ciṟantiruntamiyiṉ maṟavar eṉṟum, mullinilattār kuṟiñcikkum marutattiṟkum iṭiyilirun tamiyāl iṭiyar eṉṟum āvik kāttalāl āyar eṉṟum, marutanilattār uḻavic ciṟappāyc ceytatāl uḻavar eṉṟum, neytalnilattār paṭavut toḻil ceytamiyiṉ paṭavar allatu paravar eṉṟuṅ kūṟappaṭṭār.
paṭaku paṭavu. ka-va, pōli. paṭavar-paravar-paratavar. ṭa - ra, pōli. paṭavar cenniṟamāyiruppatāl cempaṭavar eṉappaṭṭār. paratavar virittal tiripu.
nakaravāḻkki nili
māntar marutanilattiṟku vantapiṉ niliyāyk kuṭiyirukkat toṭaṅkiṉar; ataṉāl kuṭiyāṉavar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. niliyāyk kuṭiyiruntatiṉāl, marutanilattūrkaḷ mūtūrum pērūrum nakarum mānakaru māyiṉa. marutanilattūrkaḷ piṟa nilattūrkaḷiṉum palavakiyiṟ ciṟantiruntamiyāl, ūr eṉṟē yaḻikkappaṭṭaṉa.
ūr nakar eṉṉum peyarkaḷ, mutalil taṉi vīṭṭiyum, piṉpu, vīṭṭut tokutiyāṉa ūriyum kuṟittaṉa. nakar eṉpatu piṟkālattil pērūrukku variyaṟukkappaṭṭatu.
niliyāṉa vāḻkkiyālum, māntar perukkālum, mutaṉ mutal nakarattilēyē nākarikam ciṟappāyt tōṉṟiṟṟu. nākarikam eṉṉum collum nakaram eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟum piṟantatē. atu muṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭatu.
araciyal
māntar niliyāyuḷḷa iṭattil āṭporuṭ pātukāp piruppatum, niliyillāta iṭattil aviyillātiruppatum iyalpu. marutanilattil māntar niliyāyiruntamiyiṉ, āṭporuṭ pātukāppiṟku mutalāvatu kāvalum, piṉpu ūrāṇmi nāṭṭāṇmikaḷum, ataṉ piṉ araciyalum tōṉṟiṉa.
kuṭikaḷi oru mantipōṉṟum aracaṉi ataṉ mēyp paṉ pōṉṟuṅ karutiṉar. ataṉāl, aracaṉ kōṉ eṉappaṭ ṭāṉ. avaṉ kiyil, araciṟku aṭiyāḷamāka oru mēyppaṉ kōluṅ koṭukkappaṭṭatu. atu nērāyiruntamipaṟṟic ceṅkōl eṉappaṭṭatu. ceṅkōl cemmiyāṉa aracāṭcikku aṭiyāḷam.
kō = pacu. kō + aṉ = kōvaṉ - kōṉ - kō(ṉ). kōṉ eṉṉum col āveṉṉum poruḷil āriya moḻikaḷiṟ peruvaḻakkāka vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
Swed. - Dan. ko, Du. koe, Skt. go, Ger. kuh, Ice. kyr, Irish - Gael. bo, L. bos, Gk. bous.
kō eṉṉuñ col, āveṉṉum poruḷil tamiḻil vaḻaṅkāmiyālum, āriyattil vaḻaṅkuvatiṉālum ati āriyaccol eṉṟu koḷḷaṟka. pacuvik kuṟikka, ā, peṟṟam eṉa ēṉiyiru coṟkaḷ vaḻaṅkuvatiṉālēyē, kō eṉṉum col ap poruḷil vaḻakkaṟṟateṉka.
kōviyuṭiyavaṉ kōṉ. aṉ īṟṟil akaram tokkatu. o.nō: yāvar - yār.
kōvaṉ, kōṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ iyaṟporuḷil iṭiyaṉiyum, uruvakap poruḷil aracaṉiyuṅ kuṟikkum.
kōvaṉiri mīṭṭaṉaṉ (cīvaka. 455) eṉpatil iṭiyaṉiyum, kōvaṉum makkaḷum (cīvaka. 1843) eṉpatil aracaṉiyum kōvaṉ eṉṉuñ col kuṟittatu.
kōṉ kōṉār (uyarvuppaṉmi) eṉṉum peyarkaḷ iṭiyarkkuk kuṭippeyarāy vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. kōṉ eṉṉum col, ceṅkōṉ kaṭuṅkōṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷil 
aracaṉik kuṟittatu.
aracaṉ eṉṉum poruḷil, kōṉ eṉṉum peyarē īṟu keṭṭuk kō eṉṟākum. talikkaḻakak kālat taracar peyar ceṅkōṉ kaṭuṅkōṉ eṉṟu vaḻaṅkiṉatiyum, kaṭikkaḻakak kālattaracar peyar (pālipāṭiya peruṅ) kaṭuṅkō, iḷaṅkō (aṭikaḷ) eṉṟu vaḻaṅkiṉatiyum, kō eṉṉum peyar pacupporuḷuk kēṟpatiyum kōṉ eṉṉum peyar aracaṉukkum iṭiyaṉukkumaṉṟip pacuvuk kēlāmiyiyum nōkkuka.
kō eṉpati, ā mā eṉpavaṟṟōṭu cērttu,
āmā kōṉav vaṇiyavum peṟumē	(naṉ. 248)
eṉṟu pavaṇantiyār kūṟiyatu tavaṟākum.
kō eṉṉum peyar peṟṟattiṟku vaḻakkaṟṟup pōṉamiyiṉ, kōṉ eṉpatiṉ īṟukeṭṭa vaṭivam, 
aracaṉik kuṟikkumpōtu mayakkattiṟ kiṭamilli.
tā eṉṉum col āriya moḻikaḷil vaḻaṅkiṉum, tamiḻukku eṅṅaṉam uriyatō, aṅṅaṉamē kō eṉṉuñ collum uriya teṉka. iṉṉum itaṉ mayakkut teḷiya, ip puttakattiṉ iṟutiyiṟ kāṇka.
araciyalpaṟṟic cila tamiḻccoṟkaḷ āriya moḻikaḷil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. avi aracu, pāḻi mutaliyaṉa. aracu eṉṉuñ colliṉ varalāṟu piṉṉark kūṟappaṭum.
pāḻi = nakar. Gk. polis, a city. ic colliliruntē, police (the system of regulations of a city), policy (manner of governing a city or 
nation), political (pertining to policy), politics (science of government), 
polity (civil constitution) mutaliya coṟkaḷ piṟakkum.
toḻiṟperukkamum kulap pirivum
marutanilattil mutalāvatu uḻavar eṉṉum orē vakuppār iruntaṉar. piṉpu, muṟiyē vaṇikam araciyal tuṟavu eṉpaṉa paṟṟi, av vakuppiṉiṉṟum pirintu pōṉavar, vāṇikar aracar antaṇar eṉappaṭṭār. uḻavar mutaliya nāṟpālum piṟkālat til ēṟṟattalimi muṟiyil talimāṟṟik kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. uḻavar kaṭiyiṟ kūṟappaṭṭamiyiṉ kaṭiyar eṉappaṭṭār.
kol, necavu mutaliya kittoḻilpaṟṟip piṉpu uḻavar kulattiṉiṉṟē palar pirintaṉar.
uḻavar piṟarinōkka, vēḷāṇmiyil (upacārattil) ciṟantiruntamiyiṉ vēḷāḷar eṉappaṭṭār. avaruḷ vaṟiya rāyiṉār uḻutuṇpārum celvarāyiṉār uḻuvittuṇpāru māyiṉar. ivarē niṟampaṟṟi muṟiyē karuṅkaḷamar allatu kārāḷar eṉṟum, veṇkaḷamar allatu veḷḷāḷar eṉṟuṅ kūṟappaṭupavar. kaḷattil vēli ceypavar kaḷamar.
karuṅkaḷamarum oḻukkam, ūṇ, iṭam, paḻakkavaḻakkam mutaliyaṉapaṟṟip piṟkālattiṟ paṟpala kulamāyp pirintu pōyiṉar. iṅṅaṉamē piṟakulattārum. ivaṟṟiṉ virivi eṉatu teṉṉāṭṭuk kulamarapu eṉṉum nūliṟ kaṇṭukoḷka.
nakarattil araciyal tōṉṟiṉapiṉ tiṇimayakkam ēṟpaṭṭatu. mullinilattilirunta iṭiyarum pālinilatti lirunta kaḷḷar maṟavarum, neytalnilattilirunta cempaṭavarum, kuṟiñcinilattilirunta kuṟavarum nakarattiṟku vantu tattam toḻilic ceyvārāyiṉar. avaruṭ kaḷḷarum maṟavarum muṟiyē cōḻaṉukkum pāṇṭiyaṉukkum paṭiñarāyiṉar. paṇṭit tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ veṟṟic ciṟappiṟku ivviru kulamum peruṅkāraṇameṉpatu teṉṉāṭṭuk kulamarapil viḷakkap paṭum.
kaḷḷar taṉit tamiḻarāyiruppavum, avarip pallavareṉṟu  ōr  ayal  vakuppārākak  kūṟi varukiṉṟaṉar cilar. pallavar eṉpār,  ki.pi. 4ām nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal 9ām nūṟṟāṇṭu variyum,  cōḻanāṭṭi  allatu  toṇṭi  maṇṭalatti  yāṇṭa ōr aracak kuṭumpattārēyaṉṟit taṉik kulattiṉarallar. avaruṭiya  kuṭikaḷum  paṭiñarum tamiḻarē. avarukkut taṉimoḻiyum taṉimatamumilli. tamiḻnāṭṭu moḻikaḷum mataṅkaḷumē avarkkiruntaṉavum. celvamum maṟamum paṭitta evaṉum, oru paṭiyit tiraṭṭikkoṇṭu oru nāṭṭik kippaṟṟa eṉṟum iṭamuṇṭu.
pallavarkkirunta paṭṭap peyarkaḷuḷ, toṇṭiyaṉ eṉpatum oṉṟu. pallavarkkum paṉṉūṟṟāṇṭukku muṉpē, cōḻanāṭṭiṉ vaṭapākattiṟkut toṇṭimaṇṭalam eṉṟum, ataṉ aracaṉukkut toṇṭimāṉ eṉṟum peyar vaḻaṅkiṉami perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭiyā laṟiyappaṭum. av vāṟṟuppaṭit talivaṉip pāṭiya kaṭiyalūr uruttiraṅ kaṇṇaṉār, karikāl vaḷavaṉiyum pāṭiyuḷḷār. karikāl vaḷavaṉ kālam ki.pi. 1ām nūṟṟāṇṭākum. ākiyāl, putukkōṭṭi aracarit toṇṭimāṉ eṉṉum paṭṭam paṟṟip pallava marapiṉarākak kūṟutal poruntāteṉka. mēlum toṇṭimāṉ eṉpatu taṉittamiḻc collātaluṅ kāṇka.
vaḻipāṭum matamum
vaḻipāṭāvatu oru ciṟuteyvattiyēṉum muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷiyēṉum vaṇaṅkum vaṇakkam. matamāvatu vīṭupēṟu karuti muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷi yaṭiyum peruneṟi.
mati + am = matam. matittal - karututal. kaṭavuḷippaṟṟiya matippu matamākum. matattiṟkuc camayam eṉṟum peyaruṇṭu. 
cami + am = (camiyam) - camayam. camital - pakkuvamātal. peṇṭir pūppaṭitaliyum arici cōṟātaliyum camital eṉṟu colluvatum, pakkuvamātal eṉṉuṅ karuttuppaṟṟiyē. matam āṉmākkaḷi vīṭupēṟṟiṟkup pakkuvappaṭuttalāl camayam eṉappaṭṭatu. camiyam = pakkuvamāṉa vēḷi, vēḷi. camayam pakkuvamākkum neṟi allatu koḷki. vēḷiyik kuṟikkum camiyam eṉṉum col, matattik kuṟittaṟku aikāram akaramāyiṟṟu. oru col poruḷ vēṟupaṭutaṟku eḻuttu māṟuvatu, oru colliyal nūl neṟimuṟiyākum.
kā :	paḻimi (toṉmi) - paḻami (purāṇam); mutaliyār - mutaliyōr.
	noṭippaḻami, paḻami pēcutal eṉṉum vaḻakku kaḷi nōkkuka.
vaḻipāṭu - Cults
aintiṇit teyvam
māyōṉ mēya kāṭuṟi yulakamum
 cēyōṉ mēya mivari ulakamum
 vēntaṉ mēya tīmpuṉal ulakamum
 varuṇaṉ mēya perumaṇal ulakamum
 mulli kuṟiñci marutam neytaleṉac
 colliya muṟiyāṟ collavum paṭumē	(akat. 5)
eṉpatu tolkāppiyam.
mullitteyvam māyōṉ
mā = karuppu. māyōṉ kariyōṉ. māyōṉukku māl eṉṟum peyar. māl = karuppu, mēkam, vāṉam, kariyōṉ. mullinilattil, mēlē eṅkup pārttālum nīla allatu kariya vāṉamum mēkamumāyt tōṉṟuvatālum, ānirikku vēṇṭiya pullum āyarkku vēṇṭiya vāṉa vāri allatu puṉceyp payirkaḷum vaḷarvataṟku maḻi vēṇṭiyiruppatālum, mēkatti vāṉattōṭoppak koṇṭatiṉālum, mulli nilattār taṅkaḷ teyvattik karumiyāṉateṉṟu karuti, māyōṉ eṉṟum māl eṉṟum peyariṭṭaṉar. tirumāl eṉpatil tiru eṉpatu aṭi.
mullinilattiṟkuriya kaluḻaṉi(karuṭaṉi)yum tuḷaciyi yum, muṟiyē māyōṉukkuriya ūrtiyākavum pūvākavum koṇṭārkaḷ.
kuṟiñcitteyvam cēyōṉ
cēyōṉ = civantavaṉ. cēyōṉ cēntaṉ civaṉ eṉpaṉa oruporuṭ coṟkaḷ. kuṟiñciyil mūṅkilāl aṭikkaṭi tīppaṟṟik koṇṭatiṉālum, tī añcattakkatāṉa tiṉālum. atit (teyvamāka allatu) teyva veḷip pāṭākak koṇṭu, ataṟkuc cēyōṉ eṉṟu peyariṭṭārkaḷ.
cēyōṉukkuk kuṟiñci nilattiṟkuriya mayili ūrti yākavum, cēvalik koṭiyākavum, kaṭampamalarip pūvākavuṅ koṇṭaṉar.
kōḻiyi vīṭṭil vaḷarttatu piṟkāla vaḻakkam. cūrapaṉmaṉ mayiluṅ kōḻiyumākik kantaṉukku muṟiyē ūrtiyuṅ koṭiyumāṉāṉ eṉṟatu piṟkālak kati. cēval pōr ceyvatiṟ ciṟanta paṟaviyātalāl, at toḻiliṟ ciṟanta cēyōṉukkuk koṭiyuruvamāyk koḷḷappaṭṭateṉka.
 cēyōṉukku murukaṉ, kantaṉ, āṟumukaṉ eṉṟum peyaruṇṭu. muruku eṉpatu maṇam, aḻaku, iḷami eṉṉum pala poruḷkaḷi yuṭiyatu. teyvattiṟku allatu pēykku maṇam viruppameṉṟum, malaraṇintukoṇṭāvatu malaruḷḷa iṭattilāvatu paruvappeṇkaḷ taṉiccellak kūṭāteṉṟum, oru koḷki tamiḻarkkuḷ iṉṟum iruntuvarukiṉṟatu.
teyvam iyalpāka aḻakuḷḷateṉṟu ellā matattā rālum oppukkoḷḷappaṭukiṉṟatu. itaṉāl aḻakaṉ eṉṟu tirumālukkum cokkaṉ eṉṟu civaṉukkum piṟkālattiṟ peyarkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. aḻaku eppōtum iḷamiyoṭu kūṭiyatu. kōviyil nilikaṇṭurittal eṉṉun tuṟiyi nōkkuka.
kantaṉ eṉṉum peyar, kantil (tūṇil) teyvavuruvip poṟitta allatu cetukkiya piṟkālattil tōṉṟiṉatākat terikiṉṟatu. kantiṟpāvi eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka. kantaṉiyē skantaṉ eṉṟaṉar vaṭanūlār.
āṟumukam eṉṉum peyar piṉṉark kūṟappaṭum.
marutatteyvam vēntaṉ
vēntaṉ =  aracaṉ.
nalviṉi ceytavariṉ uyirkaḷ iṟantapiṉ mēlulakat tiṟkuc cellumeṉṟum, ulakil (marutanilattil) aracaṉāyirun tavaṉ maṟumiyil mēlulakattilum aracaṉāvāṉ eṉṟum, marutanila māntar karuti, mutaṉmutal iṟanta aracaṉiyē vēntaṉ eṉṟu peyariṭṭu vaṇaṅkiṉārkaḷ. maḻi mēliruntu peyvatāl, mēlulaka vēntaṉākiya taṅkaḷ teyvattiṉiṭamiruntē varuvatākak karuti, maḻivaḷattiṟkākavum avaṉi vaḻipaṭṭārkaḷ.
nalviṉiyāvaṉa vēḷāṇmiyum pōrttoḻilum. pōrttoḻilil oruvaṉ piṟar naṉmikkeṉṟu taṉ uyirik koṭuttalāl, atu taliciṟanta vēḷāṇmiyākum. ciṟanta illaṟattārkkum pōril paṭṭa maṟavarkkum maṟumiyil vāṉulakam eṉpatu, avar vēḷāṇmiyiṟ ciṟantavar eṉṉum karuttuppaṟṟiyē,
celvirun tōmpi varuviruntu pārttiruppāṉ
 nalviruntu vāṉat tavarkku	(kuṟaḷ. 86)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar.

nīḷkaḻaṉ maṟavar celvuḻic celka	(puṟat. 93)
eṉpataṉāl, pōriliṟantavar vāṉulakam pukuvar eṉṟa koḷ ki yaṟiyappaṭum.
uḻavuttoḻil ceyyum paḷḷarum pōrttoḻiṟkuriya maṟavarum, iṉṟum taṅkaḷi intirakulattāreṉṟum, taṅkaḷ kulamutalvaṉ intiraṉeṉṟum kūṟikkoḷkiṉṟaṉar.
aracaṉiṭattil vēḷāṇmiyum pōrttoḻilum oruṅkēyuṇṭu. avaṉ immiyilum maḻikkuk kāraṇamākak karutappaṭṭāṉ.
iyalpuḷik kōlōccu maṉṉava ṉāṭṭa
 peyalum viḷiyuḷun tokku	(kuṟaḷ. 545)
 muṟikōṭi maṉṉavaṉ ceyyi ṉuṟikōṭi
 yollātu vāṉam peyar	(kuṟaḷ. 559)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
vēḷāṇmikkuc ciṟanta uḻavar kuṭiyiruppatum, ciṟanta aracu mutaṉmutal tōṉṟiyatum marutanilamē.
pālinilattu maṟavar paṭiñarākumuṉ, marutanilattu uḻavarē pōrttoḻil ceytu vantaṉar. ataṉ piṉpum uḻavar pōrttoḻili viṭṭuviṭavilli. iti,
vēntuviṭu toḻiliṟ paṭiyum kaṇṇiyum
 vāyntaṉar eṉpa avarpeṟum poruḷē	(marapu. 77)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyaṅ kūṟuvatālum, maḷḷar, maḻavar eṉṉum peyarkaṭku uḻavar maṟavar eṉṉu miru poruḷu muṇmiyālum, uḻavar kulattil oru vakuppār paṭiyāṭciyeṉṟu peyar peṟṟiruppatiṉālum, mēṉāṭṭilum kīḻnāṭṭilumirunta paṇṭi amaranāyaka (Feudalism)KiwahY« aṟiyalām.
paṇṭik kālattil aracariyum teyvamāka vaṇaṅkiṉar eṉpatu, iraṇiyaṉ, nēpukāttēccār mutaliyōr carittirat tālum, aracaṉum teyvamu mirukkumiṭam kōyil eṉṟu kūṟappaṭuvatiṉālum, aracaṉukkum aracikkum tēvaṉ tēvi eṉṉum peyarkaḷ vaḻaṅkuvatālum, tiruvāyk kēḷvi tirumantira vōli mutaliya aracaka aluvaṟ peyarkaḷālum, piṟavaṟṟālum aṟiyappaṭum.
vēntaṉ eṉṉum peyariyē, intiraṉ eṉṟu āriyar moḻipeyarttukkoṇṭaṉar. intiraṉ = aracaṉ. narēntiraṉ, mirukēntiraṉ, kavīntiraṉ mutaliya peyarkaḷi nōkkuka.
nūṟu kutirivēḷvi vēntaṉ (intiraṉ) patavikkut takutiyāka āriyappaḻaminūl kūṟum. kutirivēḷvi ceypavaṉ aracaṉē.
kaṭikkaḻakak kālamvari vēntaṉ vaḻipāṭu tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ ciṟantiruntateṉpatu, cilappatikārattālum maṇimēkaliyālum piṟanūl kuṟippukaḷālum aṟiyappaṭum.
(āyarpāṭiyil vēntaṉ vaḻipāṭṭi niṟuttiya) kaṇṇaṉ vaḻipāṭu varavarat tamiḻnāṭṭil valuttatiṉālum, nakara māntaruḷ uḻavar ciṟupāṉmiyāṉatiṉālum, vēntaṉ maḻivaḷam oṉṟē tarum ciṟuteyvamātalālum, civam tirumāliyam eṉṉum iraṇṭum vīṭupēṟṟiṟkuriya perumataṅkaḷāy vaḷarntuviṭṭami yālum, vēntaṉ vaṇakkam perumpālum niṉṟuviṭṭatu. itupōtu orōvōriṭattuḷḷa maḻit teyvavuruvamē paṇṭi vēntaṉ vaḻipāṭṭiṉ aṭiyāḷamāyuḷḷatu.
marutanilat teyvattukkup paṇṭirunta perumiyi, akattiyar vēntaṉ civikiyic cumantatākat tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇamum kumarimaliyi makēntirameṉṟu vaṭanūlkaḷum aintiratti viṇṇavar kōmāṉ viḻunūl eṉṟu cilappati kāramum kūṟuvatum, vēntaṉ viḻāvi oru cōḻaṉ niṟuttiyatāl pukār kaṭalvāyppaṭṭateṉṟa koḷkiyum viḷakkum.
vēntaṉukkuc cēṇōṉ purantaraṉ eṉṟum peyaruṇṭu. cēṇ uyaram. cēṇulakattaracaṉ cēṇōṉ. vāṉulakip purantarupavaṉ purantaraṉ. purantarutal kāttal.
neytalnilat teyvam vāraṇaṉ

ākavē, vāraṇaṉ eṉṉum peyarē varuṇaṉ eṉṟu vaṭamoḻiyil tirikkappaṭṭateṉka.
pālinilat teyvam koṟṟavi
pālinilam mutuvēṉiṟkālattāl tōṉṟuvatu.
koṟṟam  + avvi = koṟṟavvi - koṟṟavi. koṟṟam = veṟṟi. avvi ammi eṉpataṉ pōli, piṟkālattil pāṭṭiyik kuṟittatu.
pālinila māntarākiya kaḷḷar maṟavarukku, avaratu pōrt toḻilil koṟṟattit tarupavaḷ koṟṟavi.
koṟṟavikku ammi, māyōḷ, kāḷi, aṅkāḷammi, māri, piṭāri, kaṉṉi, kumari, pakavati mutaliya piṟa peyarkaḷumuṇṭu.
ammi = tāy, peṇteyvam. mutuvēṉiṟkālattil veppattiṉāl tōṉṟum vicūrinōy, ammiyāl tōṉṟuvatākak karutappaṭṭu ammi eṉappaṭṭatu. akkālattil taḻikkum vēppiliyum ammikkukantatākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu.
ammi (amma) eṉpatiṉ tiripē ammaṉ, ampā (va.) eṉpavi.
māyōḷ = kariyaḷ. māmi - karumi.
kaḷ - kāḷam - kāḷi. kaḷ - karuppu, kaḷḷam, kaḷaṅkam, kāḷāṉ (black mushroom) mutaliya coṟkaḷ karuppu eṉṉum mūlapporuḷik koṇṭavi.
kāḷi vaṇakkam muṟkālattil tamiḻakattil mikac ciṟan tiruntatu. vaṅkāḷattil uḷḷa kāḷikkōṭṭam eṉṉum iṭappeyar iṉṟu kāḷikkaṭṭam - Calcutta - kalkattā eṉṟu tirintu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. kōṭṭam = araṇ, kōyil. kōṭu + am = kōṭṭam. kōṭutal - vaḷital. kōṭiya (vaḷinta) matilāṟ cūḻappeṟṟatu kōṭṭam (toḻilākupeyar). kōṭu +ai = kōṭṭi. tamiḻar allatu tirāviṭar paṇṭu vaṭintiyā vari paraviyiruntaṉar eṉpataṟkuk kāḷikkōṭṭamum oru cāṉṟām.
kāḷi kūḷi (pēy)kaḷiṉ talivi. pēyik karuppeṉṟum iruḷeṉṟum kūṟuvatu ulaka vaḻakku.
am + kāḷam (allatu kāḷi) + ammi = aṅkāḷammi.
māri = cāvu. kāḷi aḻipput teyvamākak karutappaṭṭatāl māriyeṉappaṭṭāḷ. maṭaṅkali avaḷukku ūrtiyākak koṇṭatum ik karuttuppaṟṟiyē.
pīḻi - pīṭi + ari = pīṭāri (marūup puṇarcci).
o.nō: paṉi + aṭṭu = paṉāṭṭu. arittal aḻittal.
tuṉpattiṟkuk kāraṇamāṉavaḷē ati aḻippavaḷākavuṅ karutappaṭṭāḷ.
pīṭāri - piṭāri. piṭāri = am = piṭāram.
kaṉṉi eṉṟum iḷiyōḷā yiruppavaḷ. kumari eṉṉum peyarum ip poruṭṭē.
kum + am = kumam - kumar = tiraṭci, iḷami, kaṉṉimi.
m - r, pōli. o. nō: camam - camar.
kumarirukkuñ cacipōlvāḷ	(kuṟṟā. tarumacāmi. 47) 
kum - kumi - kuvi. kuvivu - kuvavu = tiraṭci.
kumar + aṉ = kumaraṉ. kumar + i = kumari.
kumaraṉ = tiraṇṭavaṉ, iḷiñaṉ, murukaṉ.
kumari - tiraṇṭavaḷ, iḷiyaḷ, kaṉṉi, kāḷi.
o.nō: iḷavaṭṭam = iḷiñaṉ. vaṭṭam = uruṭci. 
E. vergin, from Gk. orago, to swell.
kummi - kommi = tiraṭci. o.nō: kuṭṭu - koṭṭu.
kumaraṉ - kumāraṉ(va.). kumari - kumāri (va.) - kumāratti.
pakavati (pakavaṉ eṉpatiṉ peṇpāl) kaṉṉi eṉṉum peyarkaḷ piṉṉark kūṟappaṭum.
vaḷitaruñ celvaṉi vāḻttavu miyivatō 	(kalit. 15)
eṉpatāl, cūriyaṉum pālinilattil vaṇaṅkappaṭṭami aṟiyappaṭum. `cuṭarōṭirattal eṉṉum kōvit tuṟiyum iti yuṇarttum.
iṉi, aintiṇikkum potuvāṉa cila vaḻipāṭukaḷumuḷa. aviyāvaṉa :
(1) tīvaṇakkam
maliyil mūṅkilkaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu tēyvataṉālum, cakkimukkik kaṟkaḷi oṉṟōṭoṉṟu tēyppataṉālum tī uṇṭākiṟatu. tīkkaṭi kōlāl neruppuṇṭākkum vaḻiyi mūṅkiluracit tīppaṟṟuvatiliruntu, allatu kallic cetukkum pōtu tīppoṟi tōṉṟuvatiliruntē, mutaṉmāntar kaṇṭupiṭittirukka vēṇṭum.
poruḷkaḷ tēyvatāl uṇṭākum neruppu, tēy eṉappaṭṭatu. tēy - tēyu (va.).
tēy - tē - tī. o.nō: tēṉ - tē(ttaṭṭu) - tī(m pāl). 
tē + u = tēyvu - tēvu. tēyvu - teyvu. teyvu + am = teyvam. tēvu + aṉ = tēvaṉ.
māntaṉāl mutaṉmutal vaṇaṅkappaṭṭatu tīyātalāl, ataṉ peyarkaḷ piṟkālat teyvaṅkaṭkellām potup peyarāyiṉa. tīvaṇakkamum pēyvaṇakkamum cērntē, cēyōṉ vaṇakkam mutaṉmutal tōṉṟiṟṟu.
teyvam, tēvu, tēvaṉ.
Skt. deva; L. deus, Gk. theos, god; Ice tivi; W. duw; Gael., - Ir. 
dia; A.S. tiw; E. deity.
tiv, tivya eṉpavi tēvu eṉpataṉ tiripēyātalāl, tiv eṉpatit teyvap peyarkku mūlamākak kāṭṭuvatu tavaṟākum. iṅṅaṉam mutaṉmutaṟ kāṭṭiyatu vaṭamoḻiyāriyar. vaṭamoḻikkup piṟamoḻiyi mūlamākak kāṭṭak kūṭāteṉ patē avar nōkkam. ākiyāl, avar kūṟṟip piṉpaṟṟu vōrellām, oppiyaṉ moḻinūliyalpic cevviyā yuṇarār. vaṭamoḻi vaḻakkaṟṟa moḻiyātaliṉ, ataṉ coṟkaṭkup poruntappukalal eṉṉum muṟiyil, etiyum mūlamākak kāṭṭalām. epporuḷiyum mūlapporuḷākak kūṟalām.
tīyāṉatu poruḷkaḷi aḻittuviṭuvatāl añcattakkatu; camiyalukkum kuḷir nīkkavum utavuvatāl naṉmi ceyvatu. accamum naṉmip pēṟumē, mutaṉmutal teyva vaḻipāṭu tōṉṟiyataṟkuk kāraṇam.
tīvaṇakkam paṇṭu ellānāṭṭilu miruntatu. iṉṟum, viḷakku vaṭivil ataṉ aṭiyāḷam iruntuvarukiṉṟatu.
(2) nākavaṇakkam
intiyāviluḷḷa 280 vakip pāmpukaḷuḷ, araca nākam (king cobra), nalla pāmpu, viriyaṉ mutaliyavi peru nañcuṭiyaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ, araca nākam ulakattilēyē mikak koṭiyatu. naccuppāmpukaḷuḷ parumaṉilum ituvē periyatu. itaṟkaṭuttatē teṉkaṇṭattiluḷḷa cempūtappāmpu (giant brown snake). ivaṟṟiṉ nīḷam muṟiyē 18 aṭiyum 12 aṭiyumākum.
araca nākam acām, parmā, teṉcīṉam, malēyā, pilippiṉ tīvukaḷ mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilum vatikiṉṟatu.
pāmpukaḷuḷ mikapperiyavi teṉamerikkāviluḷḷa aṉakkoṇṭāvum (anaconda) intiyāvilum malēyāvilu muḷḷa pāntaḷumē (python). ivi 30 aṭikku mēṟpaṭṭavi; māntaṉiyum vilaṅkukaḷiyum piṭittuc ciṟitu ciṟitāy viḻuṅkupavi.
kaṭaṟpāmpukaḷum, arapikkaṭal, intiyap peruṅkaṭal, amitip peruṅkaṭal ākiya iṭaṅkaḷiltāṉ mikutiyāy vāḻkiṉṟaṉa. ākiyāl, kumarināṭu pāmpu niṟinta iṭamākum.
tirunelvēlik kōṭṭakiyiṉ kīḻppākattil, iṉṟum, nalla pāmpiṉālum viriyaṉālum kaṭiyuṇṭu makkaḷ aṭikkaṭi yiṟakkiṉṟaṉar. ākiyāl, tamiḻar nākatti vaṇaṅkiṉami viyappaṉṟu.
iṉṟum tamiḻnāṭṭilum maliyāḷa nāṭṭilum cila vīṭukaḷil nākavaṇakkam iruntuvarukiṉṟatu.
nakarvatu nākam. nakar - nākar - nākam.
E. snake; A.S. snaca from snican, to creep; Ice. snakr, snokr; Dan. snog; Swed. snok; Skt. naga.
nakattil (maliyil) uḷḷatu nākam eṉṟu vaṭanūlār kūṟuvatu cariyaṉṟu. nākam - snake (mutalviri - prosthesis).
kīḻulakattil pāntaḷ mikutiyāyiruppatāl, kīḻ eṉpati aṭiyeṉṟu niṉittukkoṇṭu, ñālattiṟkaṭiyil āticēṭaṉ (= mutaṟpāmpu) iruntu tāṅkuvatākak kūṟiṉar paḻamiyar.
kīḻnāṭṭil nākavaṇakkam mikutiyāyiruntamiyāl, piṟkālattil atic civattilum tirumāliyattilum uṭpaṭutta vēṇṭi, nākam civaperumāṉukku aṇiyākavum tirumālukkup pāyalākavuṅ kūṟappaṭṭatu.
civaperumāṉ nākattit taliyi laṇintiruppatākak kūṟuvatu, nākavaṇakkattiṉ paṇṭip perumiyik kāṭṭum.
paṇṭi ekipatiyariṉ nākavaṇakkamum, ku-eṉ-atteṉ (Khou-en-Aten) eṉṉum ekiptiya varacaṉ nākavuruvit taṉ muṭiyilamittatum13, iṅku oppunōkkattakkaṉa.
tārukā vaṉattu muṉivar cilaperumāṉmēṟ pāmpi
yēviyatākak kūṟuvatu paḻamikkaṭṭu.
(3) pēyvaṇakkam - (Demonolatry)
pē eṉpatu accattiṉāl uḷaṟum oli. pēm = accam.
pēnām urumeṉa varūum kiḷavi
 āmuṟi mūṉṟum accap poruḷa	(uri. 69)
eṉpatu tolkāppiyam.
pēpē eṉṟu uḷaṟukiṟāṉ eṉpatu vaḻakku. o.nō: 
E. babble. pē-pēy. o.nō. E. fay; Fr. fee.
pē-pēntu. pēy, pēntu eṉpavi iṭiccollākavum vaḻaṅkum. kā: pēyappēya (viḻikkiṟāṉ), pēntappēnta (viḻikkiṟāṉ).
pēntu : o.nō: Ger. feind; Dut. vijand; A.S. feond; E. fiend.
13	The Funeral Tent of an Egyptian Queen. p, 96
cēmpar akarātiyil A.S. feond, pr. p. of feon, to hate eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu; feon eṉpataṟku añcu eṉpatē mūlap poruḷā yirukkalām.
pēy = accam. accattit tarum āvi pēy eṉappaṭṭatu. pēykaḷ akālamāy iṟantōriṉ āvikaḷeṉṟum, avaṟṟiṟ pala vakiyuṇṭeṉṟum collappaṭukiṟatu.
pēykaḷi maṉavuṟutiyāṟ kaṭṭuppaṭutti, avaṟṟāṟ payaṉ koḷvatu mantiram. mantiram, māntirikam, māntirikaṉ eṉpaṉa muṟiyē mantiramoḻikkum viṉikkum viṉiñaṉuk kum vaḻaṅkum peyarkaḷ. mantiram vāymoḻi eṉṉum peyarkaḷuḷ, muṉṉatu kaṭavuḷ pēy iraṇṭiyum paṟṟiyatu; piṉṉatu kaṭavuḷiyē paṟṟiyatu.
pēykaḷukkut talivi kāḷiyātalāl, avaḷi vaḻipaṭutal aintiṇikkum potuvum māntirikarkkuc ciṟappum āyiṟṟu. māntirikaḷ avaḷi vāli (= iḷiyaḷ, kaṉṉi) eṉpar.
kaṭṭuvippittal, vēlaṉ veṟiyāṭṭu, tēvarāḷaṉ, tēvarāṭṭi eṉpavi māntirikam paṟṟiya paḻaṅ kuṟippukaḷākum.
pēykaḷil oruvaki pūtam. pūtam peruñceyal 
ceyavallatu. kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattilirunta catukkappūtattip paṟṟic cilappatikārattiṟ kāṇka.
pūtavaṇakkam paṇṭikkālattil ciṟappāyirun
tatiṉālēyē, civaperumāṉ pūtakaṇaṅkaḷukkut talivar eṉappaṭṭār.
pūtam eṉṉum peyar perumip poruḷatu. potupotu (iraṭṭik kiḷavi), potukku, pontu eṉṉum coṟkaḷi nōkkuka. pontu - pontaṉ - montaṉ. o.nō. c - C., potu - (putu) - pūtu. irumpūtu - iṟumpūtu. pūtu + am = pūtam. pēy kaḷiṟ periyatu pūtam. ulakiṉ aimperuṅ karuvip poruḷkaḷ aimpūtam eṉappaṭṭaṉa.
intiyā, ekipatu, cīṉam ākiya nāṭukaḷ paṇṭik kālattil māntirikattiṟ ciṟantiruntaṉa.
pēykaḷit teyvam eṉpatu, iruvaki vaḻakkilum toṉṟutoṭṭu iṉṟuvari yuḷḷatu.
(4) naṭukal teyvam
kāṭci kalkōḷ nīrppaṭi naṭukal
 cīrttaru marapiṟ perumpaṭi vāḻttal	(puṟat. 5)
eṉpatu tolkāppiyam.
maturivīraṉ, māṭacāmi, karuppacāmi mutaliyavi naṭukal teyvaṅkaḷē.
(5) kaṟput teyvam
kaṇṇaki varalāṟu kāṇka.
(6) teṉpulattār vaṇakkam - (Ancestor Worship)
paṇṭit tamiḻar, iṟantupōṉa tam muṉṉōrit teṉ pulattār eṉṟu peyariṭṭuc camiyam vāykkumpōtellām vaṇaṅki vantaṉar. itu muṉṉōri niṉivukūrvatum periyōrkkuc ceyyum matippumākum. itu cīṉanāṭṭil mikutiyāka vuḷḷatu.
teṉpulattār teyvam viruntokkal tāṉeṉṟāṅ 
 kimpulattā ṟōmpal tali	(kuṟaḷ. 43)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
(7) nilā vaṇakkam
nilāvum oru kālattil vaṇaṅkappaṭṭatip piṟitoḻu keṉṟal eṉṉuṅ kōvittuṟiyā laṟiyalām.
nālvēḷvi
vēḷ + vi = vēḷvi. vēṭṭal virumpal. viruppattōṭu piṟari yuṇpippatu vēḷvi.
iṇittuṇit teṉpatoṉ ṟilli viruntiṉ
 ṟuṇittuṇi vēḷvip payaṉ	(kuṟaḷ. 87)
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷil, viruntit tiruvaḷḷuvar vēḷviyeṉṟami kāṇka.
vēntaṉ, vāraṇaṉ mutaliyōrkkuc ceyyum tēva
vēḷviyum, pēykaṭkuc ceyyum pūtavēḷviyum, muṉṉōrk kuc ceyyum teṉpulattār vēḷviyum, viruntiṉarkkuc ceyyum māntar vēḷviyumeṉat tamiḻar ceytuvanta vēḷvi nāṉku. ivaṟṟoṭu maṟiyōtutalip piramayākam eṉṟu cērttu, pañcamakā yakñam eṉṟaṉar āriyar. itaṉ poruntāmiyi aṟiñar aṟika. rājacūyam, acuvamētam mutaliya āriya vēḷvi kaḷ tamiḻarkkuriyaviyalla. tamiḻaracar avaṟṟi vēṭṭatu piṟkālamākum.
matam - (Religion)
mutalāvatu, muṟkūṟiya ciṟuteyva vaṇakkaṅkaḷē tamiḻark kiruntaṉa. piṉpu avaṟṟiliruntu perunteyva vaṇakkamākiya matam tōṟṟuvikkappaṭṭatu.
kuṟiñcit teyva vaṇakkattiṉiṉṟu civamum, mullit teyva vaṇakkattiṉiṉṟu tirumāliyamum tōṉṟiṉa. pālit teyvamākiya kāḷi, civaperumāṉukkut tēviyākak koḷḷap paṭṭāḷ. marutatteyvamum neytal teyvamum kaṭikkaḻakak kālam vari vaṇaṅkappaṭṭup piṉpu kiviṭappaṭṭaṉa.
cēyōṉ eṉṉum peyariṉ maṟuvaṭivamē civam eṉpatu. civa - cē (marūu). civa + am = civam. civam + (aṉ) = civaṉ. cē + y = cēy. cē + ōṉ = cēyōṉ. cēntōṉ cēntaṉ. civaṉ, cēyōṉ. cēntaṉ eṉṉum mūṉṟum, civantavaṉ eṉṟu poruḷpaṭṭu murukaṉiyē allatu oru teyvattiyē mutalāvatu kuṟittaṉa.
cēy, cēyōṉ, kumaraṉ eṉṉum peyarkaṭku, makaṉ eṉṟu tavaṟākap poruḷkoṇṭu, civam eṉpatu cēyōṉiṉ tantiyeṉa, orē teyvatti yiraṇṭākak kūṟiviṭṭaṉar āriyar. iṅṅaṉam ceytatu, āriyatteyvamākiya ruttiraṉic civattōṭiṇittaṟkum utaviṟṟu.
murukaṉum civamum oṉṟēyeṉpati, cēyōṉ civaṉ eṉṉum iru peyarkaḷum orē poruḷuṭimiyālum, kuṟiñcikkuriya mali, kāḷi, tuṭi, maḻu, pulittōl, karittōl mutaliyavi civaṉukkuk kūṟappaṭuvatālum, kumaraṉ eṉpatiṉ peṇpāṟ peyarāṉa kumari eṉpatu civaṉ tēviyākiya kāḷiyik kuṟittalālum aṟiyappaṭum.
ticikaḷit teyvattiṟku mukaṅkaḷākavum kikaḷākavuṅ kūṟuvatu vaḻakkam. ticikaḷ mutalāvatu nāṉkeṉṟum piṉpu uyarticiyik kūṭṭi ainteṉṟum, piṉpu aṭitticiyuṅ kūṭṭi āṟeṉṟum, piṉpu nāṉku perunticiyum nāṉku kōṇatticiyumāka eṭṭeṉṟum, piṉpu ivaṟṟoṭu uyarticiyum aṭitticiyuṅ kūṭṭip patteṉṟuṅ kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
aintu ticikaḷum eṭṭutticikaḷum, muṟiyē civaperumā ṉukku aimmukaṅkaḷāvum eṭṭuk kikaḷākavum, āṟu ticikaḷā ṉavi, murukaṉukku āṟu mukaṅkaḷākavuṅ kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. āṟu mukaṅkaṭkum mukattiṟ kiraṇṭākap paṉṉirukikaḷ kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. civaperumāṉukkum āṟumukam eṉṟu oru kālattil koḷḷap paṭṭiruntami, avar tamatu āṟumukaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟum āṟupoṟi yit tōṟṟuvittu murukaṉip paṭittatākak kūṟum kanta purāṇak katiyā laṟiyalām. āṟumukatti ṣaṇmukam eṉṟu moḻipeyarttum, cuppiramaṇyaṉ, kārttikēyaṉ, kukaṉ eṉap putu vaṭamoḻip peyarkaḷiyiṭṭum, paṉimaliyil caravaṇap poykiyiṟ piṟantatākak kati kaṭṭiyum, murukaṉi āriyat teyvamākak kūṟiṉar vaṭanūlār.
muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷukkup pēraṇṭattiyē vaṭivamākak kūṟuvatu vaḻakkamātalāl, māliyil tōṉṟum cevvāṉam civaperumāṉukkuc caṭiyākavum, cevvāṉattiṟku mēlākat tōṉṟum piṟiyiyum paṉimaliyucciyil piṟakkum kaṅkiyi yum avar taliyilaṇintiruppatākavum uruvakittuk kūṟiṉar muṉṉōr.
tiṅkaḷ takkaṉ putalviyar irupatteḻuvari maṇantatākak kūṟuvatu, irāvāṉ cuṭarkaṭkut talimiyāṉatu nilā eṉpatē. uṭupati eṉṉum peyari nōkkuka.
tī mutaṉmutal vaṇaṅkappaṭṭa teyvamātalālum, iṟivaṉ perumpālum tīyāka allatu oḷiyāka veḷippaṭuva tālum, aimpūtaṅkaḷuḷ tīyē aṟivu, tūymi, naṉmi, aḻippu mutaliya palavakiyil iṟivaṉik kuṟittaṟkuc ciṟattalālum, ataṉ niṟampaṟṟiya civam eṉṉum peyarē muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuṭkuc ciṟanta peyarākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu.
civam eṉṉum col rukvētattiṟ cērkkappaṭṭatu piṟkālamākum. ac coṟku maṅkalam allatu naṉmi eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟiṉum, atuvum tirumakaḷ niṟam civappu eṉṉum koḷki paṟṟiyatē.
civaperumāṉukkuc cemmaṇiyākiya māṇikkatti uvami kūṟuvatālum, mutaṉmutal civattuṟavikaḷē nerup piṉ niṟamāṉa kāviyuṭiyi aṇintatiṉālum, civam eṉṉum collukkuc civappu eṉṉum poruḷē teḷivā yiruttalālum, civam eṉpatu tamiḻtteyvamē yātalālum, maṅkalam allatu naṉmi ceypavaṉ eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟic civam eṉṉuñ colli vaṭacollākak kūṟuvatu poruntātu.
cemmi eṉṉum niṟam paṟṟiya collukku, nērmi, nērmiyāṉa oḻukkam eṉṟu poruḷ vantatu, tuṟavikaḷ kāviyuṭi yaṇintatiṉālēyē. cemmiyāyiruttal eṉpatu, mutalāvatu ceṅkōlamāyiruttal eṉṟu poruḷpaṭṭup piṉpu, cevviyā yoḻukal eṉṟu poruḷpaṭṭatu.
civam tamiḻat teyvamēyeṉpatu, āriyar varumuṉṉamē, mokañca tārōvil civam vaḻaṅkiyatiṉālum,
mantira māmali makēntira veṟpaṉ
 maṉṉu māmali makēntira mataṉiṟ
  coṉṉa vākaman tōṟṟuvit taruḷiyum
 pāṇṭi nāṭē paḻampati yākavum
 teṉṉā ṭuṭiya civaṉē pōṟṟi
eṉṟu māṇikkavācakar kūṟuvatālum aṟiyappaṭum.
mutalāvatu oru teyvamāyirunta civattiliruntu, murukaṉ, piḷḷiyār, cāttaṉār, piravaṉ, vīrapattiraṉ eṉap pala teyvaṅkaḷ tōṟṟuvikkappaṭṭaṉa paḻamiyarāl.
piḷḷiyār eṉṟu mutalāvatu murukaṉukkup peyariṭap paṭṭatu. piṉpu āṉimukattar mūtta piḷḷiyār eṉappaṭṭār. ippōtu, mūtta piḷḷiyārē piḷḷiyār eṉappaṭukiṟār.
ōṅkāra varivaṭivam yāṉi vaṭivupō liruttalālum, kayamukaṉ eṉṉum acuraṉik kayamukattoṭu ceṉṟa civa āṟṟal koṉṟa teṉṉuṅ katiyālum, piḷḷiyār eṉṟa teyvam tōṟṟuvikkappaṭṭatu. kaṇapati (pūtakaṇaṅkaḷukkut talivar) eṉṉum peyar civaṉukkum ēṟṟalālum, civaṉukkum piḷḷiyārukkum mukkaṇ kūṟappaṭuvatālum, av viruvarum oruvarē yeṉpatuṇarappaṭum.
iṅṅaṉamē piṟateyvaṅkaḷum ovvōr kāraṇam paṟṟit tōṟṟuvikkappaṭṭaṉa vākum.
piḷḷiyār vaṇakkam kaṭikkaḻakakkālattiṟku muntiyiruntatākat teriyavilli. murukaṉukkup piḷḷiyār utaviṉatākak kantapurāṇaṅ kūṟuvatu kaḷiṟutaru puṇarcciyē yaṉṟi vēṟaṉṟu.
civaperumāṉukkup pakavaṉ, aiyaṉ eṉṟu peyarkaḷuṇ ṭāṉamiyiṉ, kāḷikkup pakavati, aiyi eṉṟu peyarkaḷuṇ ṭāyiṉa. pakavaṉ eṉṉum peyarukku aṟivu, tiru, āṭci, āṟṟal, avāviṉmi, pukaḻ eṉṉum aṟukuṇaṅkaḷi yuṭiyavaṉ eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. cilar pakam eṉpatu kuṟi (iliṅkam)yeṉṟum, pakavaṭiviṟ kuṟikkappaṭum kaṭavuḷ pakavaṉ eṉṟuṅ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. irupāṟ kūṭṭattāl uyirkaḷ tōṉṟuvatālum, kaṭavuḷiṭattil tāy kūṟum tanti kūṟum uḷḷaṉa eṉṉuṅ karuttil, civapirāṉi ammiyappaṉ, mātorupākaṉ, maṅkipaṅkaṉ mutaliya peyarkaḷāl aḻippatālum, vaḻipaṭappaṭum civavuru iliṅkappeyaruḷḷa tāyum iliṅka vaṭivamāyu miruttalālum, ellāvaṟṟiyum tōṟṟuvikkum oru mūla āṟṟalik kuṟivaṭivāl muṉṉōr kuṟippittār eṉṟu koḷḷal poruttamāṉatē.
epporuḷ ettaṉmit tāyiṉum apporuḷ
 meypporuḷ kāṇpa taṟivu.	(kuṟaḷ. 355)
kāḷi tuṭukku niliyaḷātaliṉ, aminta nilikkuriya tēvivaṭivam civaṉ eṉpatiṉ peṇpālāṉa civi eṉṉum peyarāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭatu. civiyip pārvati yeṉpar vaṭanūlār.
civi parvatavaracaṉ makaḷākip pārvatiyeṉṟu peyar peṟṟatāka oru kati kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. āṉāl, kuṟiñcit tēvi eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum malimakaḷ eṉṉum peyariyē, malikku makaḷ eṉap piṟaḻavuṇarntu ak kati kaṭṭappaṭṭatō eṉa aiyuṟak kiṭakkiṉṟatu. kalimakaḷ, malarmakaḷ, nilamakaḷ mutaliya peyarkaḷil makaḷ eṉpatu muṟikuṟikkātu peṇpāliyē kuṟittal kāṇka.  parvatavaracaṉ eṉṉum toṭarum malikaṭku aracupōṟ ciṟanta paṉimaliyiyē kuṟittaliyum, civaṉ paṇṭu kuṟiñcit teyvamā yiruntatiyum, makēntiram muḻukiya piṉ paṉimali civaṉukkuc ciṟanta iṭamākak koḷḷappaṭṭa miyiyum nōkkuka. ākavē, maliyaracaṉ peṟṟa makaḷ eṉpatu paṉimali aṭinta allatu taṅkiya peṇṭeyvam eṉṟē poruḷpaṭuvatu kāṇka.
civaṉiyamum (civamum) māliyamum (viṇavamum) vevvēṟu camayaṅkaḷā yirukka avaṟṟi āriyar muttirumēṉi (tirimūrtti)k koḷkipaṟṟi oṉṟāyiṇittatāl, cila putuk  karuttukaḷik koḷḷa vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu. aviyāvaṉa :
araṉ = tēvaṉ. ip peyariṉ varalāṟu piṉṉark kūṟappaṭum.
(1) civaperumāṉukku aḻippum tirumālukkuk kāppum toḻilāka vakuttal.
tīppōlpavaṉ civaṉ eṉṟum mēkam pōlpavaṉ tirumāl eṉṟuṅ koṇṭamiyāl, muṟiyē avaṟṟiṉ toḻilāṉa aḻippum kāppum av viruvarkkum kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. tī āṇṭaṉmiyum nīr peṇṭaṉmiyuṅ kuṟittaṟkēṟṟal kāṇka.
(2) ariyaraṉ (hariharaṉ allatu caṅkara nārāyaṇaṉ) eṉṟa vaṭivam amittal.
(3) tirumāli malimakaḷukku aṇṇaṉ eṉṟal.
iv viruvar niṟamum karumi yeṉpatiyum nōkkuka. karumi, nīlam, pacumi eṉṉum mūṉṟum oṉṟākavē koḷḷap paṭum.
(4) piramaṉ tirumāliṉ makaṉ eṉal.
im muṟi kāṭṭaṟkē, piramaṉukkut tirumāliṉ tiruvuntit toṭarpu kūṟappaṭṭatu.
tirumāliyarāyt terikiṉṟa tolkāppiyar kālatti liruntu tirumāl taṉit teyvamākap pirittuk kūṟappaṭṭu, atiliruntu tirumāliyamē ciṟantu varukiṉṟatu eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ :
(1) tolkāppiyam
i.	māyōṉ mēya eṉṟa nūṟpāvil māyōṉi muṟkūṟutal.
ii.	māyōṉ mēya maṉperuñ ciṟappiṟ
	 ṟāvā viḻuppukaḻp pūvi niliyum	(tol. 1006)
	eṉṟu maṉṉarkkut tirumāli uvami kūṟal.
(2) tirukkuṟaḷ
i.	tāmarik kaṇṇāṉ eṉṟum, aṭiyaḷantāṉ tāaya tellā moruṅku eṉṟum, tirumāli vitappāyc cuṭṭal.
ii.	tēviṟ ṟirumāl eṉṟu kavicākarap peruntēva ṉār kūṟal (tiruvaḷḷuva veṇpāmāli).
(3) cilappatikāram
tirumālippaṟṟiya pakutikaḷi mikac ciṟappāyk kūṟal.
(4)	tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakku ari ōm nama eṉṟu toṭaṅkiya tāl arivariyeṉappaṭutal.
(5)	itupōtu cila civarum iramam nāmam aṇital.
civaṉ tirumāl eṉṉum tamiḻt teyvaṅkaḷōṭu, muṟiyē ruttiraṉ viṣṇu eṉṉum āriyatteyvaṅkaḷi iṇittuviṭṭaṉar āriyar. āriyarkku ruttiraṉ puyaṟkāṟṟut teyvamum viṣṇu cūriyat teyvamumākum.
kāḷikkuc ciṟanta iruppiṭam cuṭaliyātalāṉum, pēykaṭkut tuṇaṅkik kūttu uriyatātalāṉum, kāḷiyiṉ kaṇavaṉāṉa civaperumāṉ cuṭaliyāṭi yeṉappaṭṭār.
civaperumāṉuṭiya muttoḻiliyē tirukkūttāka uruvakittaṉar uyarntōr.
