(4)	kāṭṭiya vērccol tavaṟāyiruttal.
(5)	pala teṉcoṟkaḷi vaṭacoṟkaḷākavum piṟacoṟka ḷākavum kāṭṭiyiruttal.

moḻipporuṭ kāraṇam viḻippat tōṉṟā	(uri. 98)
eṉṟār tolkāppiyar.
viḻippa eṉpatu viḻittamaṭṭil allatu pārttamaṭṭil eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum. viḻippat tōṉṟā eṉpataṟku “beyond ascertinment” eṉṟu, palkalikkaḻaka akarātip patippāciriya rākiya uyartiru viyāpurip piḷḷiyavarkaḷ kūṟiyiruppatu tavaṟākum.

kaḷāyk  kāṭṭappaṭṭiruppataṟku, iru kāṭṭut tarukiṉṟēṉ.
(i) mayil < mayūra (va.)
mi = karuppu. mayil = kariyatu, pacciyāṉatu. karumi nīlam pacciyeṉpaṉa iruvaki vaḻakkilum oṉṟākak kūṟappaṭu vatuṇṭu. pacci mayil nīlakkalāpam eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷi nōkkuka. kāḷi, nīli eṉpaṉa koṟṟaviyiṉ peyarkaḷ. tirumāliṉ niṟam karumi nīlam pacumi eṉa muttiṟattiṟ kūṟappaṭum. nīlaccēliyik karuppuccēli yeṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku. caṟṟup paciya veṇṇiṟak kāḷiyi mayili eṉpar uḻavar. mayil teṉṉāṭṭiṟkuc ciṟappāyuriya kuṟiñcip paṟaviyākum. mayil eṉṉum tamiḻccollē mayūra eṉṟu vaṭacollil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟateṉka.
(ii) vaṭavi < vaṭavā (va.)
vaṭaturuvattil cila camiyaṅkaḷil tōṉṟum oḷi vaṭavi yeṉappaṭṭatu. vaṭakkiliruntu varum kāṟṟum vaṭavi vaṭanti eṉappaṭum. vaṭavi oḷi allatu tī. āṅkilattil Aurora Borealis eṉṟaḻikkappaṭum.
Arora Borealis, the northern aurora or light; L. aurora, light; 
borealis, northern - boreas, the north wind.
kirēkkar vaṭakōṭiyil vāḻnta oru vakuppāri Hyper- boreans eṉṟaḻittaṉareṉṟum. ap peyar malikkappālar eṉṟu poruḷpaṭumeṉṟum, Boreas eṉpatu vaṭakāṟṟiṉ peyareṉṟum, atu mutalāvatu malikkāṟṟu eṉṟē poruḷ paṭṭateṉṟum, Boros eṉpatiṉ poruḷ maliyeṉṟum, mākkacu mullar eḻutiya moḻinūṟ kaṭṭurikaḷ eṉṉum nūliṉ iraṇṭām pākattil, 8ām 9ām pakka aṭikkuṟippil kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
itaṉāl boros allatu boreas eṉpatu poṟi eṉṉum tamiḻccollē yeṉṟum. Hyperborean eṉpatu upparp poṟiyaṉ eṉṟamiyum tamiḻttoṭar eṉṟum terikiṉṟatu.

ulaka mutaṉmoḻik koḷki
1. māntaṉ tōṉṟiyatu kumarināṭāyirukkalām eṉpatu
itaṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷum kāraṇaṅkaḷum:
 (1)	kumari nāṭṭiṉ paḻimi.
 (2)	hekkēl skiḷēṟṟar mutaliyōr ilemuriyā māntaṉ tōṉṟiya iṭamākak kūṟiyiruttal.
 (3)	kumarināṭṭu moḻiyiṉ toṉmiyum muṉmiyum.
 (4)	teṉṉāṭṭup peruṅkaṭalkōṭ kati ulaka muḻutum vaḻaṅkal.
 (5)	makkaḷ kiḻakkiliruntu vantār eṉṟu yūta carittiraṅ kūṟal.1
 (6)	toṉmarapiṉarāṉa māntar perumpālum teṉ ñālattiliruttal.
 (7)	kumarināṭirunta iṭam ñālattiṉ naṭumiyamā yiruttal.
 (8)	kuḷiriṉum vemmiyē makkaṭkēṟṟal.
 (9)	teṉñālattiṉ vaḷami.
ulakattiṟ kiṭikkum poṉṉum, vayiramum, perum pālum teṉṉāppirikkā, teṉṉintiyā, teṉkaṇṭam 
(Australia) ākiya iṭaṅkaḷilēyē eṭukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
(10) mutal māntaṉ vāḻakkūṭiya kaṉimarakkā (ētēṉ) teṉñālak kuṟiñci nāṭukaḷilēyē kāṇak kūṭiyatā yiruttal.
1	paḻiya ēṟpāṭu mutaṟputtakam, 11 : 1
kuṟippu : kiṟittava viṭiyūḻiyar kāṉāṉ nāṭṭi ñālattiṉ miyam eṉṟu kūṟuvatu tavaṟākum. annāṭu naḷḷiki (Equator)¡F vaṭakkē 30ām 40ām pākikaṭ kiṭiyiluḷḷatu. kumarināṭṭiṭamō naḷḷikiyiṉ mēlēyō uḷḷatu. mēlum, paṇṭi ñālattil teṉpākattilēyē nilam mikkirunta teṉṟum, kāṉāṉ nāṭṭu nilam nīrkkīḻ iruntateṉṟum aṟiyavēṇṭum.
ātiyākamattil māntaṉ paṭippi yūta carittirattōṭu iṇittuk kūṟiyirukkiṟatu.
yūtar mutalmakkaḷvakuppā rallar eṉpataṟkuk kāraṇaṅkaḷ:
(i)	paḻiya ēṟpāṭṭil 4000 āṇṭuc caritiyē kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷami.
itupōtu 6000 āṇṭukaṭku mēṟpaṭṭa maraṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉavākac collappaṭukiṟatu. tamiḻmoḻi tōṉṟiya kālam evvakiyiṉum ki.mu. 5000 āṇṭukaṭkup piṟpaṭātu.
(ii)	kāyīṉ taṉṉip palar kalleṟivār eṉṟu kūṟiyiruttal.
(iii)	makkaḷ kiḻakkiliruntu vantāreṉṟu yūta carittiraṅ kūṟal.
(iv)	yūtar tēva puttirarik kaṇṭāreṉal.
tēva puttirareṉṟu ulakil collattakkavar veḷḷiyarāṉa āriyar. āriyarit tēvareṉṟu collattakkavar teṉṉāṭṭi liruntu ceṉṟa karupparāyiruntiruttal vēṇṭum.

kā : āp (appaṉ), ām (ammi), nūṉ (mīṉ), vāv (vaḷivu), mēm (mēkam = nīr), pē (vāy). mēkam eṉpatu mēlēyuḷḷa nīr eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum tamiḻccollē. mē (mēl) + kam (nīr) = mēkam.

tuḷḷatu. āṉāl, eḻukōḷkaḷik kaṇṭupiṭittapiṉ, avaṟṟiṉ peyarāl uṇṭāṉa vāram eṉṉum aḷavu piṟkālattatu.

kuṟippu : (1) arakku, arakkam, arattam, alattam, alattakam, iratti, ilanti mutaliya teṉcoṟkaḷi nōkkiṉ, ar allatu ir eṉṉum oru vērccol civappup poruḷi yuṇarttuvatu teḷivākum. arukkaṉ, aruṇam, aruṇaṉ mutaliya (vaṭa) coṟkaḷum iv vēriṉiṉṟē piṟantaṉavākum. iṅṅaṉam pala vaṭa teṉ coṟkaḷ orē mūlattaṉa.
(2) kappu = marakkiḷi. kappil vāḻvatu kappi. kōṭu vāḻ kuraṅku (marapiyal, 13) eṉṟār tolkāppiyarum.
(3) kuru = veppam, veppattāl tōṉṟum koppuḷam, oḷi, oḷivaṭivāṉa āciriyaṉ. kuru = civappu. kuruti, kurutik kāntaḷ, kurutivāram eṉṉum coṟkaḷi nōkkuka. kuruvuṅ keḻuvum niṟaṉā kummē (uri. 5) eṉṟār tolkāppiyar. kuruttal tōṉṟutal. kuruppatu kuruttu. kuru - uru. kurumam - urumam.
(4) akam = avviṭam, avvulakam, vīṭu, uḷ, uḷḷam, (nāṉ). o.nō: ikam = ivviṭam, ivvulakam, ikaparam eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka. vīṭu = tuṟakkam, illam. il = vīṭu, uḷ. uḷ = uṭpuṟam, maṉam. uḷ + am = uḷḷam - uḷam. uḷḷam eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum akam eṉṉum tamiḻccolliyē, nāṉ eṉṉum poruḷil vaḻaṅkiṉar vaṭamoḻiyāriyar. āṉmā eṉṉuñ colli, ātmaṉēpata, ātmanivētaṉam mutaliya toṭarkaḷil taṉṉik kuṟikka vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka. akam x puṟam.

intiya āriyaratu moḻi intiyāviṟku varumuṉ ippōtuḷḷa niliyililli. ilattīṉ, kirēkkam, jermāṉiyam eṉṟa mūṉṟu moḻikaṭkum neruṅkiya niliyilēyē yiruntatu; appōtu āriyam eṉṉum potup peyarēyaṉṟi oru vitappup peyarum ataṟkilli.

intustāṉiyil 48. it tokikaḷ mākkacu mullar kūṟiyaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟiraṇṭu kūṭiṉum kuṟiyiṉum ivaṟṟik kūṟiyatiṉ payaṉ māṟātiruttal kāṇka.
iṅkuk kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, vaṭamoḻi ulaka mutaṉmoḻi yāvatiṉum, oṭṭakam ūciyiṉ kātil nuḻivatu eḷitāyirukku meṉka.
tirāviṭam ciṟiya kuṭumpamēyāyiṉum, pala kāraṇampaṟṟit taṉittuk kūṟappaṭaṟ kēṟṟatākum.
muṭivu
tamiḻē ulaka mutal ilakkiyamoḻi. itaṉperumi ceṉṟa 2000 āṇṭukaḷāka maṟipaṭṭukkiṭantu, iṉṟu moḻinūṟ
kaliyāl veḷḷiṭimaliyāy viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. paṇṭit tamiḻarkkum, iṟṟit tamiḻarkkum ellāt tuṟikaḷilum āṉikkum pūṉikkum uḷḷa vēṟupāṭākum.
tamiḻi muṉṉōr centamiḻākavum taṉittamiḻākavumē vaḷarttaṉar. aṅṅaṉamē iṉimēlum vaḷarkka vēṇṭum. vaḷarppu muṟi tamiḻukkum piṟamoḻikaṭkum vēṟupaṭṭatākum. piṟamoḻikaḷil koṭuvaḻakkukaḷellām cevvaḻakkākum; tamiḻilō koṭuvaḻakkukaḷ koḷḷappaṭātu. eṉṟum centamiḻē koḷḷappaṭum.
kā : āṅkilattil r, l cila coṟkaḷil olikkappaṭā viṭṭālum kuṟṟamilli; tamiḻilō avarkaḷ eṉpati avaka eṉṟolittāl kuṟṟamām. intiyil collīṟṟu ṉakara mey arimeyyā yolippatu kuṟṟamaṉṟu; tamiḻil aṅṅaṉam olippatu kuṟṟam. teluṅkil pappu eṉpatu kuṟṟamaṉṟu; tamiḻilō kuṟṟam.  iṅṅaṉamē piṟavum paṟpala vaḻakkukaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭil vaḻaṅkiṉālum, centamiḻiyē aḷaviyākak koṇṭatiṉālēyē tamiḻ itunāḷ varikkum perumpālum tiriyātu vantirukkiṉṟatu.
tamiḻ kaṭaṉcoṟkaḷāl taḷarntataṉṟi vaḷarntataṉṟu. kaṭaṉkōṭalāl ōr ēḻikku naṉmi; āṉāl celvaṉukkō iḻivu. atupōl kaṭaṉcoṟkaḷāl piṟamoḻikku vaḷarcci; tamiḻukkō taḷarcci. mutalāvatu, piṇikku nōy eṉṟu tamiḻccolmaṭṭum tamiḻnāṭṭil vaḻaṅkiyatu; piṉpu āriyam vantapiṉ viyāti eṉṉum col vaḻaṅkiṟṟu; ataṉ piṉ āṅkilam vantapiṉ cīkku eṉṉum col vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. ituvē tamiḻukkup piṟamoḻiyā luṇṭākum vaḷarcci; iṉi inti variṉ pīmāri eṉṉum  collum vaḻaṅkumpōlum!

mati (nilā) eṉṉum col mata eṉṉum collaṭiyāyp piṟantatu. matamata eṉpatu uṇarcciyiṉmiyik kuṟikkum oru kuṟippuccol. mata - matavu = mayakkam, pētimi.
matavē maṭaṉum valiyu mākum eṉṟār tolkāppiyar.
mata - matam - matar = mayakkam. matam - mattam = mayakkam, pittiyam. nilaviṉāl mayakkam uṇṭākum eṉṟoru paṇṭik karuttup paṟṟi, nilā matiyeṉappaṭṭatu. matam - mataṉ = vali.
o.nō: cantirarōkam = pittiyam. Lunacy (insanity), from luna, the moon. mayakkam taruvatiṉālēyē, tēṉuṅ kaḷḷum matam eṉṟum matu eṉṟuṅ kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.

A.S. metan, E. mete, Skt. miti. mati - (matu) - mattu - maṭṭu.

Moon, lit. the ‘measurer of time’ found in all the Teut. languages, also in O.Slav, menso, mensis, Gk. mene, all from root ma, to measure eṉṟār cēmparārum.
